ได้ Skill แล้ว ทีนี้สร้างทั้ง 3 ไฟล์พร้อมกันเลยครับ
<details type="tool_calls" done="true" id="toolu_01AxHqpFLftGL3X5Y9UeoUBL" name="SCGC-doc-gen-mcp_generate_word" arguments="&quot;{\&quot;title\&quot;: \&quot;PLATFORM_RULES\&quot;, \&quot;category_label\&quot;: \&quot;EA_PLATFORM \\u00b7 CONSTITUTION v1.0\&quot;, \&quot;subtitle\&quot;: \&quot;Governance Document for Modular MT5 EA Development Framework\&quot;, \&quot;date_label\&quot;: \&quot;June 2026 \\u00b7 Quant Systems Architecture\&quot;, \&quot;author\&quot;: \&quot;EA_PLATFORM Architecture | June 2026\&quot;, \&quot;content\&quot;: \&quot;# 1. Core Platform Philosophy\\n\\n&gt; EA_PLATFORM is an EA factory, not a holy grail EA. Every decision must serve robustness, maintainability, and portfolio-level survivability \\u2014 not optimizer fantasy.\\n\\n| Principle | Statement |\\n|-----------|-----------|\\n| Factory mindset | Build reusable infrastructure; swap strategies, not infrastructure |\\n| Robustness first | A system that survives beats a system that wins once |\\n| Risk engine always on | No trade executes without passing risk validation |\\n| Diagnostics mandatory | All development work runs with diagnostics active |\\n| Modular by default | Every capability is a replaceable module |\\n| Survivability &gt; theoretical profit | DD control and portfolio survival take priority over max return |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 2. Development Principles\\n\\n- Every EA must be built from EA_TEMPLATE \\u2014 no standalone monolithic builds\\n- Signal logic is always isolated in Signal Engine \\u2014 never mixed into Entry/Exit logic\\n- Infrastructure modules (Risk, Diagnostics, Logging) are shared across all EAs\\n- No feature is added outside the feature gate system\\n- New EA concepts start as Signal Engine replacements only \\u2014 core infrastructure stays unchanged\\n- Code must be readable by AI tools (Claude, Codex) \\u2014 avoid implicit state and side effects\\n- Each module has exactly one responsibility \\u2014 no overlapping logic between modules\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 3. Risk Governance Rules\\n\\n| Rule | Requirement |\\n|------|-------------|\\n| Max risk per trade | Defined in Risk Engine input \\u2014 never hardcoded in Signal/Entry |\\n| Max daily DD | Risk Engine enforces halt \\u2014 EA stops opening positions |\\n| Max total DD | Risk Engine triggers emergency close or halt |\\n| Position sizing | Always calculated by Risk Engine \\u2014 never by Signal or Entry |\\n| Lot size floor | Minimum 0.01 lot \\u2014 never below broker minimum |\\n| Correlation check | Required before adding exposure in same direction |\\n| Leverage cap | Maximum allowed leverage defined at platform level |\\n| Risk Engine bypass | PROHIBITED under any condition |\\n\\n**Hard Rule:** If Risk Engine reports an error or is uninitialized, EA must halt and log \\u2014 not trade.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 4. Recovery Rules\\n\\n| Rule | Default State |\\n|------|--------------|\\n| Recovery module | OFF |\\n| Hedge module | OFF |\\n| Basket module | OFF |\\n| Recovery trigger | Manual enable via feature gate only |\\n| Recovery logic | Lives in Recovery Engine \\u2014 never in Entry/Exit |\\n| Max recovery lot multiplier | Defined in Recovery Engine input \\u2014 platform cap applies |\\n| Recovery activation condition | Defined drawdown threshold only \\u2014 no time-based triggers |\\n\\n&gt; Recovery is an optional tool, not a fallback for bad signal quality. Never enable recovery to compensate for a strategy that fails baseline validation.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 5. Optimization Rules\\n\\n| Rule | Policy |\\n|------|--------|\\n| Optimizer-only promotion | PROHIBITED \\u2014 every parameter set must pass OOS test |\\n| Optimization target | Use custom criteria (e.g. Profit Factor + Sharpe) \\u2014 never pure profit |\\n| Overfit detection | Walk-forward or Monte Carlo required before promotion |\\n| Parameter count | Minimize \\u2014 fewer free parameters = more robust |\\n| Step size in optimizer | Use realistic steps \\u2014 avoid micro-optimization |\\n| Re-optimization trigger | Only on structural market change \\u2014 not on routine DD |\\n| Optimization environment | Strategy Tester only \\u2014 never on live account |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 6. Validation Pipeline Rules\\n\\nEvery EA candidate must pass all stages in order. No stage can be skipped.\\n\\n| Stage | Description | Pass Criteria |\\n|-------|-------------|---------------|\\n| 1. Baseline | Single-pass default parameters, 2\\u20135 years in-sample | Positive PF &gt; 1.3, DD &lt; 25% |\\n| 2. Single Test | One specific optimized parameter set | PF &gt; 1.4, Sharpe &gt; 0.8 |\\n| 3. OOS Test | Untouched out-of-sample period (min 6 months) | PF &gt; 1.2, no equity cliff |\\n| 4. Forward Test | Live demo or small live forward | Matches backtest behavior within tolerance |\\n| 5. Portfolio Review | Check correlation and sizing with existing EAs | No excess correlated DD |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 7. OOS Rules\\n\\n- OOS period must be set aside **before** any optimization begins\\n- Minimum OOS length: 6 months; preferred: 12 months\\n- OOS data must never be used for parameter tuning\\n- If OOS fails: discard candidate \\u2014 do not re-optimize to pass OOS\\n- OOS environment: same broker, same symbol, same spread model as intended deployment\\n- OOS pass \\u2260 deployment approval \\u2014 forward test still required\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 8. Portfolio Philosophy\\n\\n| Principle | Policy |\\n|-----------|--------|\\n| Correlation limit | Maximum 2 highly correlated EAs active simultaneously |\\n| Per-EA risk cap | Max risk per EA defined at portfolio level |\\n| Total portfolio DD limit | Monitored by PortfolioGuardianEA |\\n| Symbol diversification | Preferred across XAUUSD, BTCUSD, EURUSD at minimum |\\n| Strategy type diversification | Mix trend + mean reversion \\u2014 avoid all-trend portfolio |\\n| EA promotion to live | Only after full validation pipeline passes |\\n| Portfolio rebalancing | Quarterly review minimum |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 9. Module Architecture Philosophy\\n\\n- Each module is a single `.mqh` include file or `.mq5` EA file\\n- Modules communicate via defined interfaces \\u2014 no direct struct access between modules\\n- No circular dependencies \\u2014 dependency graph must be a DAG\\n- Optional modules (Recovery, Hedge, Basket, Telegram) are feature-gated\\n- Core modules (Risk, Entry, Exit, Signal, Diagnostics, Logging) are always present\\n- Module state is owned by the module \\u2014 no external mutation\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 10. Feature Gate Rules\\n\\n| Gate Name | Controlled Module | Default |\\n|-----------|------------------|---------|\\n| ENABLE_RECOVERY | Recovery Engine | false |\\n| ENABLE_HEDGE | Hedge Engine | false |\\n| ENABLE_BASKET | Basket Engine | false |\\n| ENABLE_TELEGRAM | Telegram Hooks | false |\\n| ENABLE_DIAGNOSTICS | Diagnostics | true (dev), configurable (live) |\\n| ENABLE_LOGGING | Logging | true |\\n\\n- Feature gates are `input bool` parameters in EA_TEMPLATE \\u2014 never compile-time defines\\n- Disabled modules must still compile without error\\n- All gate checks must be explicit: `if (!ENABLE_RECOVERY) return;`\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 11. Diagnostics and Logging Requirements\\n\\n**Diagnostics** (development-phase active, production-optional):\\n- Trade signal reason and score\\n- Risk calculation values (lot size, SL distance, risk %)\\n- Position state at each OnTick\\n- Module initialization status\\n\\n**Logging** (always active):\\n- All trade opens with full parameter snapshot\\n- All trade closes with P&amp;L and reason code\\n- All Risk Engine halts with timestamp and trigger condition\\n- All errors and warnings with module source tag\\n\\n**Format:** `[TIMESTAMP][MODULE][LEVEL] message` \\u2014 machine-parseable\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 12. Naming Conventions\\n\\n| Entity | Convention | Example |\\n|--------|-----------|---------|\\n| EA file | `EA_StrategyName.mq5` | `EA_TrendFollow_XAUUSD.mq5` |\\n| Module file | `ModuleName_vX.mqh` | `RiskEngine_v1.mqh` |\\n| Input variable | `inp_ModuleName_Parameter` | `inp_Risk_MaxLotSize` |\\n| Global variable | `g_ModuleName_Variable` | `g_Risk_DailyDD` |\\n| Function | `ModuleName_Action()` | `Risk_CalculateLot()` |\\n| Constants | `UPPER_SNAKE_CASE` | `MAX_DAILY_DD_PCT` |\\n| Boolean gates | `ENABLE_FeatureName` | `ENABLE_RECOVERY` |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 13. Folder Conventions\\n\\n```\\nEA_PLATFORM/\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 DOCS/\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 PLATFORM_RULES.md\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 EA_TEMPLATE_SPEC.md\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 CORE_MODULE_ROADMAP.md\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 CORE/\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 RiskEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 EntryEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 ExitEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Diagnostics_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 Logging_v1.mqh\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 SIGNAL/\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Signal_TrendEMA.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 Signal_MeanRev_BB.mqh\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 OPTIONAL/\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 RecoveryEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 HedgeEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 BasketEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 TelegramHooks_v1.mqh\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 PORTFOLIO/\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 PortfolioGuardianEA.mq5\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 TEMPLATE/\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 EA_TEMPLATE.mq5\\n\\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 ARCHIVE/\\n    \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 [deprecated versions]\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 14. AI Workflow Conventions\\n\\n| AI Tool | Responsibility |\\n|---------|---------------|\\n| Claude | Architecture design, spec writing, logic review, interface definition |\\n| Codex | Code generation from spec, boilerplate, module skeleton, refactor |\\n| Human | Review, validation decisions, deployment approval |\\n\\n- Claude produces architecture docs \\u2192 human reviews \\u2192 Codex implements\\n- Never deploy Codex output without human review of logic and risk sections\\n- AI-generated code must be tagged with `// AI-GENERATED [DATE]` comment on first line\\n- AI must never modify Risk Engine without explicit human approval in session\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 15. Codex Safety Rules\\n\\n- Codex must receive module spec before generating code \\u2014 no open-ended generation\\n- Codex must not generate complete EA from scratch \\u2014 generate module by module\\n- Codex must not modify RiskEngine without explicit instruction per session\\n- Codex output must be reviewed for: hardcoded values, bypass patterns, missing null checks\\n- Codex must use naming conventions from Section 12 \\u2014 reject outputs that deviate\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 16. MT5 Operational Constraints\\n\\n| Constraint | Rule |\\n|-----------|------|\\n| OnTick safety | All heavy logic in OnTimer or OnTradeTransaction \\u2014 not raw OnTick |\\n| Sleep() usage | Prohibited in EA \\u2014 use timer-based patterns |\\n| Magic number | Unique per EA, defined in EA_TEMPLATE input |\\n| Order comment | Standard format: `EA_NAME|SIGNAL_ID|VERSION` |\\n| Broker dependency | Avoid broker-specific functions \\u2014 use standard MT5 API |\\n| Multi-symbol EA | Use symbol parameter input \\u2014 not hardcoded |\\n| Testing mode | `MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER)` check required in Diagnostics init |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 17. Hard Reject Conditions\\n\\nAn EA candidate is immediately rejected (non-negotiable) if any of the following apply:\\n\\n- Risk Engine bypassed or missing\\n- OOS test never performed\\n- Promoted based on optimizer results alone\\n- Recovery logic embedded in Entry or Exit Engine\\n- Lot size hardcoded outside Risk Engine\\n- DD in backtest exceeds platform risk policy\\n- Module dependency cycle exists in codebase\\n- Magic number conflict with existing deployed EA\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 18. Future Scaling Philosophy\\n\\n- EA_PLATFORM is designed for 1-person operation now, scalable to small team later\\n- Infrastructure must not require refactor to add new strategy \\u2014 only new Signal module\\n- Portfolio Guardian grows from manual monitoring to automated enforcement over time\\n- AI workflow matures: Claude for architecture \\u2192 Codex for implementation \\u2192 automated test runner\\n- Do not build enterprise tooling prematurely \\u2014 add complexity only when friction demands it\\n- Version all modules \\u2014 never overwrite, always increment version number on structural change\\n\\n---\\n\\n*EA_PLATFORM PLATFORM_RULES.md \\u2014 Constitution v1.0 | June 2026*\\n*This document governs all EA development within EA_PLATFORM. All team members and AI tools operating on this codebase must comply.*\\n\&quot;, \&quot;confidentiality_level\&quot;: \&quot;internal\&quot;}&quot;" result="&quot;\n\n---\n### ✅ Ready\n📁 **PLATFORM_RULES_1780726753.docx**\n🔗 [Download](https://scgc-ailylab.scg.com/mcp/docsgenerate/files/8e26b3dbde1052fa7bf136385e5b96b4.docx?exp=1780762753&amp;sig=572a4f5fd074172ddb980b0b4dbd64847e484b6204c2221c88e6449666ae889b)\n---\n&quot;" files="" embeds="&quot;&quot;">

<details type="tool_calls" done="true" id="toolu_01RJdMFjnFyJk4XBqcipEYHm" name="SCGC-doc-gen-mcp_generate_word" arguments="&quot;{\&quot;title\&quot;: \&quot;EA_TEMPLATE Architecture Specification\&quot;, \&quot;category_label\&quot;: \&quot;EA_PLATFORM \\u00b7 TEMPLATE SPEC v1.0\&quot;, \&quot;subtitle\&quot;: \&quot;Reusable Modular Framework Skeleton for MT5 EA Development\&quot;, \&quot;date_label\&quot;: \&quot;June 2026 \\u00b7 MQL5 Framework Architecture\&quot;, \&quot;author\&quot;: \&quot;EA_PLATFORM Architecture | June 2026\&quot;, \&quot;content\&quot;: \&quot;# 1. Overview\\n\\n&gt; EA_TEMPLATE is not a single strategy EA. It is a reusable framework skeleton \\u2014 a structured container that any signal logic can be plugged into without rebuilding infrastructure.\\n\\n**Goal:** Create a new EA by replacing only the Signal Engine. All other modules are shared infrastructure.\\n\\n**Target symbols:** XAUUSD, BTCUSD, EURUSD\\n**Target strategies:** Trend following, Mean reversion, Grid, Partial hedge\\n**Platform:** MetaTrader 5 (MT5) \\u2014 MQL5\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 2. Folder Structure\\n\\n```\\nEA_PLATFORM/\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 TEMPLATE/\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 EA_TEMPLATE.mq5           \\u2190 Master EA file (do not modify directly)\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 CORE/\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 RiskEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 EntryEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 ExitEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Diagnostics_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 Logging_v1.mqh\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 SIGNAL/\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Signal_Interface.mqh      \\u2190 Abstract interface definition\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Signal_TrendEMA.mqh       \\u2190 Example: EMA crossover\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 Signal_MeanRev_BB.mqh     \\u2190 Example: Bollinger Band reversion\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 OPTIONAL/\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 RecoveryEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 HedgeEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 BasketEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 TelegramHooks_v1.mqh\\n\\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 EA/\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 EA_TrendEMA_XAUUSD.mq5    \\u2190 Derived EA (copies template, sets signal)\\n    \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 EA_MeanRev_BTCUSD.mq5     \\u2190 Derived EA\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 3. File Structure \\u2014 EA_TEMPLATE.mq5\\n\\n```\\nEA_TEMPLATE.mq5\\n\\u2502\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 #include section\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 CORE/RiskEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 CORE/EntryEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 CORE/ExitEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 CORE/Diagnostics_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 CORE/Logging_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 SIGNAL/Signal_Interface.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 [conditional] OPTIONAL/RecoveryEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 [conditional] OPTIONAL/HedgeEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 [conditional] OPTIONAL/BasketEngine_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 [conditional] OPTIONAL/TelegramHooks_v1.mqh\\n\\u2502\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Input Parameters (Section 4)\\n\\u2502\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Global State Variables\\n\\u2502\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 OnInit()\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 OnDeinit()\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 OnTick()\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 OnTimer()\\n\\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 OnTradeTransaction()\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 4. Module Responsibilities\\n\\n| Module | Responsibility | Owns |\\n|--------|---------------|------|\\n| Signal Engine | Generate trade direction and score | Signal struct |\\n| Entry Engine | Execute trade open based on signal | Order send logic |\\n| Exit Engine | Manage SL/TP, trailing, close logic | Position management |\\n| Risk Engine | Calculate lot size, enforce DD limits | Risk state |\\n| Diagnostics | Monitor and report system state | Diagnostic output |\\n| Logging | Record all events to file | Log file |\\n| Recovery Engine | Optional martingale/grid recovery | Recovery state |\\n| Hedge Engine | Optional partial hedge positions | Hedge state |\\n| Basket Engine | Optional multi-position management | Basket state |\\n| Telegram Hooks | Optional notification relay | Message queue |\\n\\n**Hard rule:** No module may call another module&#x27;s internal functions \\u2014 only the defined interface methods.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 5. Interface Contracts\\n\\n## Signal Interface (Signal_Interface.mqh)\\n\\n```mql5\\n// Signal_Interface.mqh\\nstruct SignalResult {\\n    int     direction;     // 1=BUY, -1=SELL, 0=NONE\\n    double  score;         // signal strength 0.0\\u20131.0\\n    double  sl_price;      // suggested SL price (0 = use default)\\n    double  tp_price;      // suggested TP price (0 = use default)\\n    string  reason;        // human-readable signal reason\\n    datetime timestamp;    // signal generation time\\n};\\n\\ninterface ISignalEngine {\\n    bool          Init(string symbol, ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf);\\n    SignalResult  Evaluate();\\n    void          Reset();\\n    string        GetName();\\n};\\n```\\n\\n## Risk Interface\\n\\n```mql5\\nstruct RiskResult {\\n    double  lot_size;      // calculated lot\\n    bool    trade_allowed; // false = halt\\n    string  halt_reason;   // populated if trade_allowed = false\\n    double  daily_dd_pct;  // current daily DD %\\n    double  total_dd_pct;  // current total DD %\\n};\\n\\ninterface IRiskEngine {\\n    bool        Init(double account_balance);\\n    RiskResult  CalculateLot(double sl_distance_points);\\n    bool        CheckHalt();\\n    void        UpdateState();\\n    void        Reset();\\n};\\n```\\n\\n## Entry Interface\\n\\n```mql5\\ninterface IEntryEngine {\\n    bool  Init(string symbol, int magic);\\n    bool  OpenTrade(SignalResult &amp;signal, RiskResult &amp;risk);\\n    bool  IsPositionOpen();\\n    ulong GetLastTicket();\\n};\\n```\\n\\n## Exit Interface\\n\\n```mql5\\ninterface IExitEngine {\\n    bool  Init(string symbol, int magic);\\n    void  ManagePositions(SignalResult &amp;signal);\\n    bool  CloseAll(string reason);\\n    void  UpdateTrailingStop();\\n};\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 6. State Machine Structure\\n\\nEA_TEMPLATE operates as an explicit state machine. Each state has defined allowed transitions.\\n\\n```\\nStates:\\n  INIT        \\u2192 system initializing\\n  READY       \\u2192 all modules OK, waiting for signal\\n  SIGNAL      \\u2192 signal detected, evaluating entry conditions\\n  IN_TRADE    \\u2192 position open, exit logic active\\n  HALTED      \\u2192 risk limit triggered, no new trades\\n  ERROR       \\u2192 module error, requires restart\\n  CLOSING     \\u2192 all positions closing (deinit or emergency)\\n\\nTransitions:\\n  INIT        \\u2192 READY     (all modules init OK)\\n  INIT        \\u2192 ERROR     (any module init fails)\\n  READY       \\u2192 SIGNAL    (SignalResult.direction != 0)\\n  SIGNAL      \\u2192 IN_TRADE  (entry executed)\\n  SIGNAL      \\u2192 READY     (entry rejected by Risk)\\n  IN_TRADE    \\u2192 READY     (position closed normally)\\n  IN_TRADE    \\u2192 HALTED    (DD limit hit)\\n  READY       \\u2192 HALTED    (DD limit hit pre-entry)\\n  HALTED      \\u2192 READY     (manual reset or new session)\\n  ANY         \\u2192 ERROR     (unhandled exception)\\n  ANY         \\u2192 CLOSING   (OnDeinit called)\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 7. Event Lifecycle\\n\\n| MT5 Event | EA_TEMPLATE Action |\\n|-----------|-------------------|\\n| OnInit() | Init all modules in dependency order; set state = INIT; run self-check; set state = READY or ERROR |\\n| OnTick() | Update market data; check state machine; route to OnTimer logic if needed |\\n| OnTimer() | Run full tick logic: Diagnostics.Update \\u2192 Risk.UpdateState \\u2192 Signal.Evaluate \\u2192 Entry/Exit routing |\\n| OnDeinit() | Set state = CLOSING; close positions if configured; flush Logging; send Telegram deinit message |\\n| OnTradeTransaction() | Update position tracking; trigger Exit.UpdateTrailingStop; log transaction |\\n\\n**Rule:** Heavy logic runs in OnTimer \\u2014 not in raw OnTick. OnTick only updates price cache and triggers timer if needed.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 8. Trade Lifecycle\\n\\n```\\n1. OnTimer fires\\n2. Diagnostics.Update() \\u2014 record system state\\n3. Risk.UpdateState() \\u2014 recalculate DD, check halt condition\\n4. if Risk.CheckHalt() \\u2192 state = HALTED, return\\n5. Signal.Evaluate() \\u2192 SignalResult\\n6. if SignalResult.direction == 0 \\u2192 READY, return\\n7. if Entry.IsPositionOpen() \\u2192 route to Exit.ManagePositions(), return\\n8. Risk.CalculateLot(sl_distance) \\u2192 RiskResult\\n9. if !RiskResult.trade_allowed \\u2192 log reason, return\\n10. Entry.OpenTrade(signal, risk) \\u2192 position opens\\n11. Log.Record(trade_open_event)\\n12. if ENABLE_TELEGRAM \\u2192 Telegram.SendTradeOpen()\\n13. state = IN_TRADE\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 9. Feature Gate System\\n\\nAll feature gates are `input bool` parameters \\u2014 never compile-time defines.\\n\\n| Gate | Module | Default | Notes |\\n|------|--------|---------|-------|\\n| `inp_ENABLE_RECOVERY` | RecoveryEngine | false | Requires explicit human enable |\\n| `inp_ENABLE_HEDGE` | HedgeEngine | false | Requires explicit human enable |\\n| `inp_ENABLE_BASKET` | BasketEngine | false | Requires explicit human enable |\\n| `inp_ENABLE_TELEGRAM` | TelegramHooks | false | Requires bot token in inputs |\\n| `inp_ENABLE_DIAGNOSTICS` | Diagnostics | true | Disable for live production if needed |\\n| `inp_ENABLE_LOGGING` | Logging | true | Always true in development |\\n\\n**Pattern:**\\n```mql5\\nif (inp_ENABLE_RECOVERY) {\\n    g_recovery.ManageRecovery(last_ticket);\\n}\\n```\\n\\nDisabled module code must still compile \\u2014 guard with `if` checks, not `#ifdef`.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 10. Input Parameter Philosophy\\n\\nInput parameters follow strict namespacing to prevent conflicts across modules.\\n\\n```\\nGroup: \\\&quot;=== EA IDENTITY ===\\\&quot;\\ninp_EA_Name         string   \\\&quot;EA_TEMPLATE\\\&quot;\\ninp_EA_Magic        int      12345\\ninp_EA_Version      string   \\\&quot;1.0\\\&quot;\\n\\nGroup: \\\&quot;=== RISK ENGINE ===\\\&quot;\\ninp_Risk_MaxRiskPct        double  1.0\\ninp_Risk_MaxDailyDDPct     double  5.0\\ninp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct     double  15.0\\ninp_Risk_MaxLotSize        double  1.0\\ninp_Risk_MinLotSize        double  0.01\\n\\nGroup: \\\&quot;=== SIGNAL ENGINE ===\\\&quot;\\ninp_Signal_Timeframe       ENUM_TIMEFRAMES  PERIOD_H1\\ninp_Signal_FastPeriod      int     20\\ninp_Signal_SlowPeriod      int     50\\ninp_Signal_MinScore        double  0.6\\n\\nGroup: \\\&quot;=== ENTRY ENGINE ===\\\&quot;\\ninp_Entry_SlippagePts      int     10\\ninp_Entry_MaxSpreadPts     int     30\\n\\nGroup: \\\&quot;=== EXIT ENGINE ===\\\&quot;\\ninp_Exit_SL_Points         int     150\\ninp_Exit_TP_Points         int     300\\ninp_Exit_TrailingEnabled   bool    false\\ninp_Exit_TrailingStart_Pts int     100\\ninp_Exit_TrailingStep_Pts  int     20\\n\\nGroup: \\\&quot;=== FEATURE GATES ===\\\&quot;\\ninp_ENABLE_RECOVERY        bool    false\\ninp_ENABLE_HEDGE           bool    false\\ninp_ENABLE_BASKET          bool    false\\ninp_ENABLE_TELEGRAM        bool    false\\ninp_ENABLE_DIAGNOSTICS     bool    true\\ninp_ENABLE_LOGGING         bool    true\\n\\nGroup: \\\&quot;=== OPTIONAL: RECOVERY ===\\\&quot;\\ninp_Recovery_MaxMultiplier double  2.0\\ninp_Recovery_TriggerDDPct  double  3.0\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 11. Diagnostics Structure\\n\\nDiagnostics runs every timer cycle and records to both chart comment and log file.\\n\\n```\\nDiagnostic Output Format:\\n\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\n[EA_NAME v1.0] [SYMBOL] [TIMEFRAME]\\nState    : IN_TRADE\\nSignal   : BUY | Score: 0.82 | EMA Cross\\nRisk     : Lot=0.05 | DailyDD=1.2% | TotalDD=4.3%\\nPosition : Ticket=123456 | Open=1850.20 | SL=1835.00 | TP=1880.40\\nGates    : Recovery=OFF | Hedge=OFF | Basket=OFF | Telegram=OFF\\n\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\n```\\n\\nDiagnostic levels: `DEBUG`, `INFO`, `WARN`, `ERROR`\\nDiagnostics module must never affect trade logic \\u2014 read-only access only.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 12. Logging Structure\\n\\nLog file naming: `EA_NAME_SYMBOL_YYYYMMDD.log`\\n\\nLog entry format:\\n```\\n[2026.06.06 09:32:14][RISK][INFO] DailyDD=1.2% TotalDD=4.3% AllowedLot=0.05\\n[2026.06.06 09:32:15][ENTRY][INFO] BUY opened Ticket=123456 Lot=0.05 Price=1850.20 SL=1835.00 TP=1880.40\\n[2026.06.06 09:32:15][TELEGRAM][INFO] TradeOpen notification sent\\n[2026.06.06 10:45:22][EXIT][INFO] Ticket=123456 closed Price=1875.10 PnL=+124.50 Reason=TP_HIT\\n[2026.06.06 10:45:22][RISK][WARN] DailyDD limit approach 4.8% of 5.0% max\\n```\\n\\nRequired log events:\\n- Module init (success/fail)\\n- Risk state update (every N ticks)\\n- Trade open/modify/close\\n- Risk halt trigger\\n- Signal evaluation (score and direction)\\n- All errors\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 13. Recovery Integration Model\\n\\n```\\nRecovery activation conditions:\\n  1. inp_ENABLE_RECOVERY == true\\n  2. Current loss position exists\\n  3. Loss exceeds inp_Recovery_TriggerDDPct\\n\\nRecovery module responsibilities:\\n  - Track recovery sequence number\\n  - Calculate recovery lot (within platform max multiplier)\\n  - Define recovery SL/TP levels\\n  - Report recovery state to Diagnostics\\n\\nRecovery module must NOT:\\n  - Override Risk Engine lot calculation directly\\n  - Open positions without Risk Engine approval\\n  - Exceed inp_Recovery_MaxMultiplier\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 14. Basket Integration Model\\n\\nBasket Engine manages multiple correlated positions as a group.\\n\\n```\\nBasket state:\\n  - basket_id: unique per basket instance\\n  - positions[]: list of tickets in basket\\n  - total_lots: sum of all basket lots\\n  - basket_pnl: aggregate P&amp;L\\n  - close_trigger: aggregate TP/SL condition\\n\\nBasket close modes:\\n  - ALL_TP: close all when basket reaches TP\\n  - PARTIAL: close profitable legs, hold others\\n  - EMERGENCY: close all on DD trigger\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 15. Hedge Integration Model\\n\\nHedge Engine opens counter-direction positions as optional insurance.\\n\\n```\\nHedge activation:\\n  - Only when inp_ENABLE_HEDGE == true\\n  - Only on explicit signal from Signal Engine OR manual trigger\\n  - Hedge lot = fraction of original position (input-defined ratio)\\n\\nHedge lifecycle:\\n  - Open hedge \\u2192 track separately from main position\\n  - Close hedge when main position TP hit OR hedge SL hit\\n  - Log hedge separately in Logging module\\n\\nHedge must NOT:\\n  - Auto-trigger without defined condition\\n  - Exceed Risk Engine lot limits (combined)\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 16. Telegram Integration Hooks\\n\\nTelegram sends notifications \\u2014 it does not control EA behavior.\\n\\n```\\nHook points:\\n  - OnInit complete (EA started)\\n  - Trade opened\\n  - Trade closed (with P&amp;L)\\n  - Risk halt triggered\\n  - Daily summary (configurable interval)\\n  - OnDeinit (EA stopped)\\n\\nTelegram module must NOT:\\n  - Block trade execution if message fails\\n  - Store trade state (read from Logging only)\\n  - Receive commands that modify EA behavior (no remote control)\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 17. Validation Hooks\\n\\nValidation runs at OnInit \\u2014 all hooks must pass before EA enters READY state.\\n\\n| Hook | Check |\\n|------|-------|\\n| Symbol valid | Symbol exists and tradeable |\\n| Spread check | Current spread within inp_Entry_MaxSpreadPts |\\n| Account check | Account balance sufficient for min lot |\\n| Risk module | RiskEngine.Init() returns true |\\n| Magic conflict | No open positions with same magic from another EA |\\n| Historical data | Sufficient bars available for signal calculation |\\n| Broker mode | Hedging account mode confirmed if Hedge enabled |\\n\\nIf any validation hook fails: state = ERROR, log reason, disable trading, alert Diagnostics.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# 18. Suggested Compile and Build Workflow\\n\\n```\\nStep 1: Copy EA_TEMPLATE.mq5 \\u2192 EA/EA_StrategyName_Symbol.mq5\\nStep 2: Set inp_EA_Name, inp_EA_Magic (unique)\\nStep 3: Replace #include Signal line with target signal module\\nStep 4: Adjust input group \\\&quot;=== SIGNAL ENGINE ===\\\&quot; parameters\\nStep 5: Compile \\u2014 expect zero errors, zero warnings\\nStep 6: Run Strategy Tester (single pass, default params)\\nStep 7: Check Diagnostics output and log file\\nStep 8: Run baseline backtest (2\\u20135 years)\\nStep 9: If baseline passes \\u2192 proceed to optimization\\nStep 10: OOS test on untouched data\\nStep 11: Forward test on demo\\nStep 12: Deploy to live with minimum lot\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Module Dependency Map\\n\\n```\\nEA_TEMPLATE.mq5\\n    \\u2502\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Logging_v1.mqh          (no dependencies)\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Diagnostics_v1.mqh      \\u2190 Logging\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 RiskEngine_v1.mqh       \\u2190 Logging, Diagnostics\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 Signal_Interface.mqh    (no dependencies)\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 [Signal_XXXX.mqh]       \\u2190 Signal_Interface\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 EntryEngine_v1.mqh      \\u2190 RiskEngine, Logging\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 ExitEngine_v1.mqh       \\u2190 RiskEngine, Logging\\n    \\u2502\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 [RecoveryEngine_v1.mqh] \\u2190 RiskEngine, EntryEngine, Logging\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 [HedgeEngine_v1.mqh]    \\u2190 RiskEngine, EntryEngine, Logging\\n    \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 [BasketEngine_v1.mqh]   \\u2190 ExitEngine, Logging\\n    \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 [TelegramHooks_v1.mqh]  \\u2190 Logging (read-only)\\n```\\n\\n**Rule:** Dependencies flow downward only \\u2014 no module depends on a module above it in this diagram.\\n\\n---\\n\\n*EA_TEMPLATE Architecture Specification v1.0 | June 2026*\\n*Do not generate full MQL5 implementation from this document alone. This is architecture and skeleton design \\u2014 implementation is module-by-module per CORE_MODULE_ROADMAP.*\\n\&quot;, \&quot;confidentiality_level\&quot;: \&quot;internal\&quot;}&quot;" result="&quot;\n\n---\n### ✅ Ready\n📁 **EA_TEMPLATE_Architecture_Specification_1780726753.docx**\n🔗 [Download](https://scgc-ailylab.scg.com/mcp/docsgenerate/files/d7f592f039cfeb37580702cc2082f1cd.docx?exp=1780762754&amp;sig=a8472755941f48f259c91146273a00dc0d1561d92956d147706dd0adc09d4c0f)\n---\n&quot;" files="" embeds="&quot;&quot;">

<details type="tool_calls" done="true" id="toolu_01HP2dJe7gz2DvjDxdzrV6Nd" name="SCGC-doc-gen-mcp_generate_word" arguments="&quot;{\&quot;title\&quot;: \&quot;CORE_MODULE_ROADMAP\&quot;, \&quot;category_label\&quot;: \&quot;EA_PLATFORM \\u00b7 BUILD ROADMAP v1.0\&quot;, \&quot;subtitle\&quot;: \&quot;Phased Build Plan for Modular MT5 EA Platform\&quot;, \&quot;date_label\&quot;: \&quot;June 2026 \\u00b7 Platform Engineering\&quot;, \&quot;author\&quot;: \&quot;EA_PLATFORM Architecture | June 2026\&quot;, \&quot;content\&quot;: \&quot;# Overview\\n\\n&gt; Build order matters more than build speed. One module built correctly unlocks the next. One module built wrong forces a full refactor.\\n\\n**Platform goal:** Modular MT5 EA framework for 1-person operation, designed to evolve over years.\\n**Build philosophy:** Core first. Optional never. Infrastructure before strategy.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase Map\\n\\n| Phase | Module | Type | Priority |\\n|-------|--------|------|----------|\\n| 1 | Logging | CORE | Critical |\\n| 2 | Diagnostics | CORE | Critical |\\n| 3 | Risk Engine | CORE | Critical |\\n| 4 | EA_TEMPLATE skeleton | TEMPLATE | Critical |\\n| 5 | Signal Interface | CORE | Critical |\\n| 6 | Entry Engine | CORE | High |\\n| 7 | Exit Engine | CORE | High |\\n| 8 | First Signal Module | SIGNAL | High |\\n| 9 | First complete EA | EA | High |\\n| 10 | PortfolioGuardianEA (manual) | PORTFOLIO | Medium |\\n| 11 | Recovery Engine | OPTIONAL | Low |\\n| 12 | Telegram Hooks | OPTIONAL | Low |\\n| 13 | Hedge Engine | OPTIONAL | Low |\\n| 14 | Basket Engine | OPTIONAL | Low |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 1 \\u2014 Logging\\n\\n**Why first:** Every other module depends on Logging. If Logging does not exist, you cannot observe what any other module does. Building without Logging = flying blind.\\n\\n## Minimum Viable Version\\n\\n- Write timestamped entries to a `.log` file\\n- Support log levels: INFO, WARN, ERROR\\n- Format: `[TIMESTAMP][MODULE][LEVEL] message`\\n- No dependencies on any other module\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] File creates on OnInit, closes on OnDeinit\\n- [ ] All log levels write correctly\\n- [ ] Works in Strategy Tester and live account\\n- [ ] Zero compilation warnings\\n\\n## Common Failure Risks\\n\\n- File handle not closed \\u2192 memory leak\\n- Tester path differs from live \\u2192 test separately\\n- Log file grows unbounded \\u2192 add daily rotation or size cap\\n\\n## Testing\\n\\n- Unit test: write 10 entries, verify file content\\n- Tester test: run 1-day backtest, check log file output\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 2 \\u2014 Diagnostics\\n\\n**Why second:** Diagnostics depends only on Logging. Once you can observe and record, you can see what every subsequent module is doing. Without Diagnostics, you cannot safely validate any module.\\n\\n## Minimum Viable Version\\n\\n- Chart comment output with EA state, signal, risk values\\n- Read-only access to other modules (no writes)\\n- Log diagnostic snapshots at configurable interval\\n- Must be disableable via feature gate\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] Chart comment updates every N ticks without lag\\n- [ ] Diagnostic output includes EA state, risk %, signal direction\\n- [ ] Gate `inp_ENABLE_DIAGNOSTICS = false` silences all output cleanly\\n- [ ] No impact on trade logic when disabled\\n\\n## Common Failure Risks\\n\\n- Diagnostics calls mutating functions accidentally \\u2192 read-only contract must be enforced\\n- Heavy chart comment refresh lags on fast symbols \\u2192 use timer, not OnTick\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 3 \\u2014 Risk Engine\\n\\n**Why third:** Risk Engine is the most critical module in the entire platform. It must exist and be validated before a single trade can be executed. Build it before Entry, before Exit, before Signal.\\n\\n## Minimum Viable Version\\n\\n- Lot size calculation from risk % and SL distance\\n- Daily DD tracking (reset at session open)\\n- Total DD tracking from session high watermark\\n- Halt flag when DD limits breached\\n- Trade allow/deny with reason string\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] Lot calculation correct across XAUUSD, BTCUSD, EURUSD\\n- [ ] Daily DD correctly resets at broker server time\\n- [ ] Halt triggers when daily DD % exceeds limit\\n- [ ] Halt triggers when total DD % exceeds limit\\n- [ ] All calculations logged via Logging module\\n- [ ] Tested with intentional DD breach \\u2014 halt confirmed\\n\\n## Common Failure Risks\\n\\n- Tick value calculation wrong on CFD pairs (BTCUSD especially) \\u2192 verify with manual calc\\n- DD tracking resets incorrectly on server reconnect \\u2192 test disconnect scenario\\n- Lot rounding not respecting broker min step \\u2192 use broker&#x27;s `SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP`\\n\\n## Testing Gate\\n\\nRisk Engine must pass all tests before Phase 4 begins. No exceptions.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 4 \\u2014 EA_TEMPLATE Skeleton\\n\\n**Why fourth:** Once Logging, Diagnostics, and Risk Engine exist, you can build the skeleton that wires them together. This skeleton is the foundation that all future EAs inherit.\\n\\n## Minimum Viable Version\\n\\n- OnInit / OnDeinit / OnTick / OnTimer / OnTradeTransaction wired\\n- State machine implemented (INIT \\u2192 READY \\u2192 HALTED \\u2192 ERROR)\\n- All module includes connected\\n- Feature gates implemented\\n- Input parameter groups defined\\n- No signal logic yet \\u2014 just the skeleton compiles and runs\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] Template compiles with zero errors and zero warnings\\n- [ ] Runs in Strategy Tester for 1 day without crash\\n- [ ] State machine transitions logged correctly\\n- [ ] Risk Engine halt triggers and logs correctly in tester\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 5 \\u2014 Signal Interface\\n\\n**Why fifth:** The interface contract must be defined before any Signal module is built. The interface is the API \\u2014 define it once, implement many times.\\n\\n## Minimum Viable Version\\n\\n- `SignalResult` struct defined\\n- `ISignalEngine` interface defined\\n- No implementation \\u2014 just the contract\\n- Documented with inline comments\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] Interface compiles as standalone include\\n- [ ] SignalResult struct covers: direction, score, sl_price, tp_price, reason, timestamp\\n- [ ] Interface methods cover: Init, Evaluate, Reset, GetName\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 6 \\u2014 Entry Engine\\n\\n**Why sixth:** Entry Engine depends on Risk Engine (for lot/halt check) and Signal Interface (for direction). Both must exist first.\\n\\n## Minimum Viable Version\\n\\n- Open BUY/SELL market order\\n- Apply SL/TP from signal or input defaults\\n- Check Risk Engine halt before sending order\\n- Check spread before sending order\\n- Log all open attempts (success and fail)\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] Opens BUY and SELL correctly in tester\\n- [ ] Rejects order when Risk Engine halt active\\n- [ ] Rejects order when spread &gt; inp_Entry_MaxSpreadPts\\n- [ ] All outcomes logged with reason\\n\\n## Common Failure Risks\\n\\n- Order rejected by broker without useful error \\u2192 log full RetCode\\n- SL/TP calculation at wrong price reference \\u2192 use Ask for BUY, Bid for SELL\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 7 \\u2014 Exit Engine\\n\\n**Why seventh:** Exit depends on Entry having opened positions. Build after Entry is validated.\\n\\n## Minimum Viable Version\\n\\n- Close position by ticket\\n- Update SL via trailing stop (if enabled)\\n- Close on TP/SL hit detection (via OnTradeTransaction)\\n- Emergency close all positions\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] Trailing stop updates correctly in tester\\n- [ ] Emergency close all closes all magic-number positions\\n- [ ] All close events logged with P&amp;L and reason\\n\\n## Common Failure Risks\\n\\n- Trailing stop modifies order with invalid price \\u2192 add price normalization\\n- Emergency close fails on partial fill \\u2192 add retry with delay\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 8 \\u2014 First Signal Module\\n\\n**Why eighth:** All infrastructure now exists. Signal is the first \\\&quot;strategy\\\&quot; layer \\u2014 it can be replaced freely once the interface is correct.\\n\\n## Recommended First Signal\\n\\n**Signal_TrendEMA.mqh** \\u2014 EMA crossover (simple, well-understood, easy to validate)\\n\\n## Minimum Viable Version\\n\\n- Fast EMA cross above Slow EMA \\u2192 BUY signal\\n- Fast EMA cross below Slow EMA \\u2192 SELL signal\\n- Score based on EMA separation\\n- Returns SignalResult correctly\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] Implements ISignalEngine interface exactly\\n- [ ] Signal evaluation does not modify any global state\\n- [ ] Signal tested in isolation (print output, no trading) for 1 week of data\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 9 \\u2014 First Complete EA\\n\\n**Why ninth:** Integration test. Wire Signal_TrendEMA into EA_TEMPLATE and run full backtest.\\n\\n## Definition of Done\\n\\n- [ ] EA compiles from EA_TEMPLATE with Signal_TrendEMA included\\n- [ ] 2-year backtest completes without crash\\n- [ ] Risk Engine halt tested intentionally\\n- [ ] Log file contains complete trade history\\n- [ ] Diagnostics output readable and accurate\\n- [ ] PF &gt; 1.0 (not optimized \\u2014 just proving system works)\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 10 \\u2014 PortfolioGuardianEA (Manual Phase)\\n\\n**Why tenth:** Before adding complexity (optional modules), establish portfolio oversight. Start manual \\u2014 automate later.\\n\\n## Manual Phase (now)\\n\\n- Spreadsheet tracking: EA name, symbol, daily DD, total DD, status\\n- Daily review ritual: check each EA&#x27;s log file\\n- Manual halt: disable EA in MT5 if DD limit approaching\\n\\n## Automated Phase (later)\\n\\n- PortfolioGuardianEA reads positions by magic number\\n- Enforces portfolio-level DD limits\\n- Sends Telegram alert if any EA exceeds threshold\\n- Does NOT close positions \\u2014 alerts only in v1\\n\\n## Definition of Done (Manual Phase)\\n\\n- [ ] Tracking spreadsheet exists and updated daily\\n- [ ] Manual halt procedure documented\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Phase 11+ \\u2014 Optional Modules\\n\\n&gt; Do NOT build optional modules until Phase 9 is complete and the first EA has been forward-tested for at least 30 days.\\n\\n| Module | Build When |\\n|--------|-----------|\\n| Telegram Hooks | First EA deployed live, want trade notifications |\\n| Recovery Engine | Core system stable, specific strategy requires recovery |\\n| Hedge Engine | Specific symbol/strategy tested and justified |\\n| Basket Engine | Running multiple concurrent positions on same symbol |\\n\\n**Rule:** Optional modules are built to solve a specific validated need \\u2014 not preemptively.\\n\\n---\\n\\n# What NOT to Build Early\\n\\n| Item | Reason |\\n|------|--------|\\n| Full MQL5 implementation of all modules at once | Creates untestable monolith |\\n| AI orchestration / automated pipeline | Premature \\u2014 not needed for Phase 1\\u20139 |\\n| PortfolioGuardianEA automation | Manual phase is sufficient and faster |\\n| Multi-EA Telegram command interface | High complexity, low value at this stage |\\n| Web dashboard | Out of scope for 1-person operation |\\n| ML signal integration | Requires stable infrastructure first |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# What Can Remain Manual Initially\\n\\n| Item | Manual Approach |\\n|------|----------------|\\n| Portfolio monitoring | Spreadsheet + daily log review |\\n| Parameter optimization | MT5 Strategy Tester optimizer |\\n| OOS test reporting | Manual record in development notes |\\n| EA promotion decision | Human judgment after validation pipeline |\\n| Correlation tracking | Manual cross-check before deploying new EA |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# AI Workflow Recommendations\\n\\n| AI Tool | Task | When |\\n|---------|------|------|\\n| Claude | Architecture review, interface design, spec writing | Before any code |\\n| Claude | Logic review of Risk Engine and Exit Engine | Before Codex implementation |\\n| Codex | Module skeleton generation from spec | After spec approved |\\n| Codex | Boilerplate: input parameter groups, struct definitions | After interface defined |\\n| Codex | Refactor: rename variables to naming convention | After module complete |\\n| Human | All validation decisions | Always |\\n| Human | Risk Engine logic approval | Always |\\n| Human | OOS test evaluation | Always |\\n\\n**Session discipline:**\\n- One module per session with Codex\\n- Always provide spec/interface before asking Codex to code\\n- Never ask Codex to \\\&quot;build the full EA\\\&quot; \\u2014 always module-by-module\\n- Review Risk Engine Codex output line-by-line before use\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Claude vs Codex Responsibilities\\n\\n| Responsibility | Claude | Codex |\\n|---------------|--------|-------|\\n| Architecture decisions | YES | NO |\\n| Interface design | YES | NO |\\n| Naming convention review | YES | NO |\\n| Module skeleton generation | Review only | PRIMARY |\\n| Boilerplate code | NO | YES |\\n| Logic implementation | Spec only | Code |\\n| Risk Engine review | YES | Implement only |\\n| Debugging | YES | YES |\\n| Spec writing | YES | NO |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Suggested Folder Evolution\\n\\n```\\nPhase 1\\u20134  (Foundation):\\nEA_PLATFORM/\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 DOCS/\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 CORE/           \\u2190 Logging, Diagnostics, Risk Engine\\n\\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 TEMPLATE/       \\u2190 EA_TEMPLATE skeleton\\n\\nPhase 5\\u20139  (First EA):\\nEA_PLATFORM/\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 SIGNAL/         \\u2190 Signal_Interface, Signal_TrendEMA\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 EA/             \\u2190 EA_TrendEMA_XAUUSD.mq5\\n\\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 BACKTEST/       \\u2190 backtest result notes\\n\\nPhase 10+  (Portfolio):\\nEA_PLATFORM/\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 OPTIONAL/       \\u2190 add only when needed\\n\\u251c\\u2500\\u2500 PORTFOLIO/      \\u2190 PortfolioGuardianEA\\n\\u2514\\u2500\\u2500 ARCHIVE/        \\u2190 deprecated versions\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Milestone Checkpoints\\n\\n| Milestone | Condition | Action |\\n|-----------|-----------|--------|\\n| M1 \\u2014 Foundation | Logging + Diagnostics + Risk Engine pass all tests | Proceed to EA_TEMPLATE |\\n| M2 \\u2014 Template | EA_TEMPLATE skeleton compiles and runs in tester | Proceed to Signal Interface |\\n| M3 \\u2014 First EA | EA_TrendEMA_XAUUSD runs full 2-year backtest | Proceed to optimization |\\n| M4 \\u2014 Validated EA | OOS test passes, forward test 30 days | Consider live deployment |\\n| M5 \\u2014 Live EA | EA running live with manual monitoring | Review optional modules |\\n| M6 \\u2014 Second EA | Second signal module integrated using same infrastructure | Validate no regression |\\n| M7 \\u2014 Portfolio | 3+ EAs running \\u2014 PortfolioGuardianEA manual phase active | Consider automation |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Module Maturity Levels\\n\\n| Level | Description | Criteria |\\n|-------|-------------|---------|\\n| L0 \\u2014 Concept | Spec written, not implemented | Spec document exists |\\n| L1 \\u2014 Skeleton | Compiles, no logic | Zero compilation errors |\\n| L2 \\u2014 Development | Logic implemented, untested | Runs in tester |\\n| L3 \\u2014 Tested | All test gates passed | Test log documented |\\n| L4 \\u2014 Validated | Used in complete EA backtest | Backtest log exists |\\n| L5 \\u2014 Production | Running on live account | Forward test &gt; 30 days |\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Operational Warnings\\n\\n&gt; **Warning 1:** Do not promote any EA to live before Phase 9 milestone is met. Partial infrastructure = unpredictable behavior.\\n\\n&gt; **Warning 2:** Recovery Engine must never be enabled before the underlying strategy passes OOS without recovery.\\n\\n&gt; **Warning 3:** Each Codex session should produce one module file. Multi-module sessions produce inconsistent code quality.\\n\\n&gt; **Warning 4:** Never run two EAs with the same magic number simultaneously \\u2014 position tracking will break silently.\\n\\n&gt; **Warning 5:** Platform rules (PLATFORM_RULES.md) take precedence over any single-session AI suggestion. Review rules before accepting architectural changes.\\n\\n---\\n\\n*CORE_MODULE_ROADMAP v1.0 | June 2026*\\n*This roadmap governs the build sequence for EA_PLATFORM. Review and update at each milestone checkpoint.*\\n\&quot;, \&quot;confidentiality_level\&quot;: \&quot;internal\&quot;}&quot;" result="&quot;\n\n---\n### ✅ Ready\n📁 **CORE_MODULE_ROADMAP_1780726754.docx**\n🔗 [Download](https://scgc-ailylab.scg.com/mcp/docsgenerate/files/89ec09f43fe86244d15ed23b3246218e.docx?exp=1780762754&amp;sig=9ffe94845451ffa854a96451947c453476ba7744d4c9a053f6d3af70ce636e72)\n---\n&quot;" files="" embeds="&quot;&quot;">

Patch EA_PLATFORM architecture docs for v1.1 consistency.  
  
Files:  
- PLATFORM_RULES.md  
- EA_TEMPLATE_SPEC.md  
- CORE_MODULE_ROADMAP.md  
  
Required fixes:  
  
1. MQL5 compatibility  
Replace C#/Java-like interface wording with MQL5-compatible contract wording.  
Use:  
- abstract base class if suitable  
- struct + required function naming convention  
- include-file contract  
Do not imply unsupported language features.  
  
2. Validation gate split  
Replace single strict pass criteria with two levels:  
A) Research Candidate Gate  
B) Promotion Candidate Gate  
  
Suggested:  
Research:  
- PF > 1.05–1.10  
- DD controlled  
- trades sufficient  
- no accounting/reporting fail  
  
Promotion:  
- PF > 1.20+  
- OOS survives  
- DD within policy  
- single test confirms optimizer  
  
3. Disabled module optimization rule  
Add:  
If a feature gate is false, all related parameters must be locked and excluded from optimization.  
  
4. Manual MT5 workflow  
Add:  
Manual MT5 run import is accepted as trusted workflow when automated launcher context differs from manual MT5.  
Reports should be collected/imported into EA_LAB.  
  
5. Active/archive simplification  
Add:  
ACTIVE contains only latest reports, current candidate sets, and summary files.  
Raw optimization artifacts and rejected runs go to ARCHIVE.  
  
Return patch summary.

The job isn&#x27;t to fill a file with content.
