## FILE 1 — `CORE/Diagnostics_Contract.mqh`

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Diagnostics_Contract.mqh                                         |
//| EA_PLATFORM · CORE · Pattern C Include-File Contract             |
//| Version    : 1.0                                                  |
//| Phase      : 2                                                    |
//| Date       : 2026.06.06                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//
// CONTRACT RULES
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. Any Diagnostics_vX.mqh MUST implement ALL functions below.
// 2. Diagnostics is READ-ONLY — it must NEVER:
//      open trades / close trades / modify positions / alter risk logic
// 3. Dependency direction: Logging ← Diagnostics (never reversed)
// 4. Prefix rules:
//      public   → Diag_
//      internal → _Diag_
// 5. Do NOT modify this file. Version-bump creates a new contract.
//
// ── COUNTER PHILOSOPHY ────────────────────────────────────────────
// Counters are unsigned integers (int, capped at INT_MAX).
// Counters only increment — never decrement.
// Counters reset only via Diag_Reset() or Diag_Init().
// All counters are named with snake_case after the Diag_ prefix.
// Counter names describe an EVENT that occurred, not a state.
//   Good : signals_buy, entries_blocked, spread_blocked
//   Bad  : is_blocked, position_count
//
// ── STATE FLAG PHILOSOPHY ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Flags are bool. They represent the CURRENT runtime state.
// Flags may be set and cleared during a session.
// Flag names use present-tense adjectives.
//   Good : trading_locked, cooldown_active, recovery_active
//   Bad  : was_locked, lock_happened
// Flags are set via Diag_SetFlag(). Never set directly from outside.
//
// ── BLOCK REASON PHILOSOPHY ───────────────────────────────────────
// Block reasons are recorded via Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_REASON).
// Each call increments the matching fixed counter.
// Block reasons are defined in ENUM_DIAG_BLOCK_REASON.
// Extending block reasons requires adding to the enum and counter
// struct — no dynamic allocation.
//
// ── EXPORT PHILOSOPHY ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Exports are ON-DEMAND only — never written per-tick or per-counter.
// Two export formats:
//   CSV  → machine-readable  → diagnostics_<ea>_<symbol>_<date>.csv
//   TXT  → human-readable   → diagnostics_<ea>_<symbol>_<date>.txt
// Export is called explicitly: Diag_ExportSummary() or Diag_Close().
// Export must be tester-safe (MQL5\Files\ path, no FILE_COMMON).
//
// ── KNOWN LIMITATION (document for Phase 3+) ─────────────────────
// Diagnostics_v1 uses static variables inside the .mqh include file.
// This is acceptable for Phase 1–2 single-include architecture.
// Phase 3+ (Risk Engine onward) will migrate to a single global
// struct (g_Diag) to prevent state collision in complex include chains.
//
// ── REQUIRED PUBLIC FUNCTIONS ─────────────────────────────────────
//
// void Diag_Init(string ea_name, string symbol)
//   Called once in OnInit() after Log_Init().
//   Resets all counters and flags to zero/false.
//   Records session-open diagnostic entry via Log_Write().
//   Safe to call again on EA re-attach (idempotent reset).
//
// void Diag_Reset()
//   Resets all counters and flags without closing file handles.
//   Use on tester rerun or manual reset mid-session.
//   Does NOT close log integration — Logging remains open.
//
// void Diag_Increment(string counter_name)
//   Increments a named counter by 1.
//   counter_name must match a defined counter field name (string key).
//   Unknown counter names are logged as WARN and ignored.
//   Capped at INT_MAX — no overflow.
//
// void Diag_SetFlag(string flag_name, bool value)
//   Sets a named runtime flag to value.
//   flag_name must match a defined flag field name.
//   Unknown flag names are logged as WARN and ignored.
//   Changes are logged via Log_Write() at DEBUG level.
//
// void Diag_RecordBlock(int block_reason)
//   Records a blocked entry event by ENUM_DIAG_BLOCK_REASON value.
//   Increments the corresponding fixed counter.
//   Logs the block reason via Log_Write() at INFO level.
//   Does NOT open/close/modify any trade.
//
// void Diag_ExportSummary()
//   Writes CSV + TXT summary files to MQL5\Files\.
//   Called on-demand — never per-tick.
//   Safe to call multiple times (overwrites previous export).
//   Logs export completion via Log_Write().
//
// void Diag_Close()
//   Called once in OnDeinit().
//   Calls Diag_ExportSummary() automatically.
//   Logs session-close summary via Log_Write().
//   Safe to call multiple times (idempotent).
//   Resets internal ready flag.
//
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#ifndef __DIAGNOSTICS_CONTRACT__
#define __DIAGNOSTICS_CONTRACT__

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ENUM — Block Reasons
// Extend here when new block types are added.
// Each value maps 1:1 to a counter field in DiagCounters struct.
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
enum ENUM_DIAG_BLOCK_REASON
{
    BLOCK_SPREAD       = 0,  // spread exceeded limit
    BLOCK_DD           = 1,  // drawdown limit active
    BLOCK_MAX_POS      = 2,  // max open positions reached
    BLOCK_COOLDOWN     = 3,  // cooldown period active
    BLOCK_RISK_HALT    = 4,  // Risk Engine halt flag
    BLOCK_NO_SIGNAL    = 5,  // signal direction = 0
    BLOCK_INVALID_LOT  = 6,  // lot calculation returned 0 or invalid
    BLOCK_OTHER        = 7   // uncategorised block — use sparingly
};

#define DIAG_BLOCK_COUNT 8   // must equal number of ENUM values

#endif // __DIAGNOSTICS_CONTRACT__
```

---

## FILE 2 — `CORE/Diagnostics_v1.mqh`

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Diagnostics_v1.mqh                                               |
//| EA_PLATFORM · CORE · Phase 2                                     |
//| Version    : 1.0                                                  |
//| Contract   : Diagnostics_Contract.mqh (Pattern C)               |
//| Depends on : Logging_v1.mqh                                      |
//| Date       : 2026.06.06                                          |
//| AI-GENERATED 2026.06.06 — review before use                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//
// PLATFORM RULES APPLIED:
//   §11 — Log integration via Log_Write()
//   §12 — Prefix: Diag_ (public), _Diag_ (internal)
//   §16 — MQL_TESTER guard; tester-safe file paths
//   §13 — Exports written to MQL5\Files\
//   ARCH REVIEW — CSV on-demand only (no per-tick I/O)
//   ARCH REVIEW — fixed counters + ENUM (no dynamic arrays)
//   ARCH REVIEW — static var pattern acceptable Phase 2;
//                 migrate to g_Diag struct in Phase 3+
//   ARCH REVIEW — Diag_RecordBlock() replaces Diag_LogBlock()
//
// READ-ONLY GUARANTEE:
//   This module contains zero order management calls.
//   No OrderSend, OrderClose, PositionOpen, PositionClose.
//   No modification of any global trade state.
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

#ifndef __DIAGNOSTICS_V1__
#define __DIAGNOSTICS_V1__

#include "Diagnostics_Contract.mqh"
#include "Logging_v1.mqh"

//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// STRUCTS
//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// DiagCounters — all runtime event counters
// Counter semantics: increment-only, reset via Diag_Reset() only.
// Phase 3+ note: migrate into g_Diag global struct.
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
struct DiagCounters
{
    // Signal events
    int signals_buy;
    int signals_sell;
    int signals_none;

    // Entry events
    int entries_attempted;
    int entries_executed;
    int entries_blocked;

    // Block counters — one per ENUM_DIAG_BLOCK_REASON value
    int block_spread;
    int block_dd;
    int block_max_pos;
    int block_cooldown;
    int block_risk_halt;
    int block_no_signal;
    int block_invalid_lot;
    int block_other;

    // Exit events
    int exits_tp;
    int exits_sl;
    int exits_manual;
    int exits_emergency;

    // Optional module events
    int recovery_activated;
    int hedge_activated;
    int basket_opened;

    // Tick / timer cycles (for session activity tracking)
    int timer_cycles;
};

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// DiagFlags — current runtime state flags
// Flags reflect CURRENT state. May be set and cleared freely.
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
struct DiagFlags
{
    bool trading_locked;
    bool cooldown_active;
    bool recovery_active;
    bool hedge_active;
    bool basket_active;
    bool spread_too_wide;
    bool dd_limit_active;
};

//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// INTERNAL STATE  (static, file-scope)
// Acceptable for Phase 2 single-include architecture.
// Phase 3+: migrate to g_Diag global struct pattern.
//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
static DiagCounters _Diag_Counters;
static DiagFlags    _Diag_Flags;
static string       _Diag_EAName   = "";
static string       _Diag_Symbol   = "";
static string       _Diag_DateTag  = "";
static bool         _Diag_Ready    = false;
static datetime     _Diag_InitTime = 0;

//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// INTERNAL HELPERS
//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Zero all counters
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
static void _Diag_ZeroCounters()
{
    _Diag_Counters.signals_buy        = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.signals_sell       = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.signals_none       = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.entries_attempted  = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.entries_executed   = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.entries_blocked    = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.block_spread       = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.block_dd           = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.block_max_pos      = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.block_cooldown     = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.block_risk_halt    = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.block_no_signal    = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.block_invalid_lot  = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.block_other        = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.exits_tp           = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.exits_sl           = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.exits_manual       = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.exits_emergency    = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.recovery_activated = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.hedge_activated    = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.basket_opened      = 0;
    _Diag_Counters.timer_cycles       = 0;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Zero all flags
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
static void _Diag_ZeroFlags()
{
    _Diag_Flags.trading_locked  = false;
    _Diag_Flags.cooldown_active = false;
    _Diag_Flags.recovery_active = false;
    _Diag_Flags.hedge_active    = false;
    _Diag_Flags.basket_active   = false;
    _Diag_Flags.spread_too_wide = false;
    _Diag_Flags.dd_limit_active = false;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Build YYYYMMDD date tag from datetime
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
static string _Diag_DateString(datetime dt)
{
    MqlDateTime s;
    TimeToStruct(dt, s);
    return StringFormat("%04d%02d%02d", s.year, s.mon, s.day);
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Build base file name (no extension)
// Pattern: diagnostics_<ea>_<symbol>_<date>
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
static string _Diag_BaseFileName()
{
    string ea  = _Diag_EAName;
    string sym = _Diag_Symbol;
    StringReplace(ea,  " ", "_");
    StringReplace(sym, " ", "_");
    return "diagnostics_" + ea + "_" + sym + "_" + _Diag_DateTag;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Return block reason string from enum value
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
static string _Diag_BlockName(int reason)
{
    switch(reason)
    {
        case BLOCK_SPREAD:      return "SPREAD";
        case BLOCK_DD:          return "DD_LIMIT";
        case BLOCK_MAX_POS:     return "MAX_POSITIONS";
        case BLOCK_COOLDOWN:    return "COOLDOWN";
        case BLOCK_RISK_HALT:   return "RISK_HALT";
        case BLOCK_NO_SIGNAL:   return "NO_SIGNAL";
        case BLOCK_INVALID_LOT: return "INVALID_LOT";
        case BLOCK_OTHER:       return "OTHER";
        default:                return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Safe increment — caps at INT_MAX, no overflow
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
static int _Diag_SafeIncrement(int current)
{
    if(current >= INT_MAX) return INT_MAX;
    return current + 1;
}

//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PUBLIC API — implements Diagnostics_Contract.mqh
//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Diag_Init
// Called once in OnInit() after Log_Init().
// Resets all state. Records session open marker.
// Safe to call again on re-attach (idempotent).
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void Diag_Init(string ea_name, string symbol)
{
    _Diag_EAName   = ea_name;
    _Diag_Symbol   = symbol;
    _Diag_InitTime = TimeCurrent();
    _Diag_DateTag  = _Diag_DateString(_Diag_InitTime);
    _Diag_Ready    = true;

    _Diag_ZeroCounters();
    _Diag_ZeroFlags();

    Log_Write("DIAG", "INFO",
        "Diag_Init  EA=" + ea_name +
        "  Symbol="      + symbol  +
        "  Time="        + TimeToString(_Diag_InitTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS) +
        (MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER) ? "  [TESTER]" : "  [LIVE]"));
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Diag_Reset
// Resets counters and flags without touching file handles or log.
// Use on tester rerun, manual mid-session reset, or EA re-attach.
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void Diag_Reset()
{
    _Diag_ZeroCounters();
    _Diag_ZeroFlags();
    _Diag_InitTime = TimeCurrent();

    if(_Diag_Ready)
        Log_Write("DIAG", "INFO",
            "Diag_Reset  counters=0  flags=false  Time=" +
            TimeToString(_Diag_InitTime, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS));
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Diag_Increment
// Increments a named counter by 1.
// Unknown names log WARN and return — no crash.
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void Diag_Increment(string counter_name)
{
    if(!_Diag_Ready) return;

    if(counter_name == "signals_buy")
        { _Diag_Counters.signals_buy        = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.signals_buy);        return; }
    if(counter_name == "signals_sell")
        { _Diag_Counters.signals_sell       = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.signals_sell);       return; }
    if(counter_name == "signals_none")
        { _Diag_Counters.signals_none       = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.signals_none);       return; }
    if(counter_name == "entries_attempted")
        { _Diag_Counters.entries_attempted  = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.entries_attempted);  return; }
    if(counter_name == "entries_executed")
        { _Diag_Counters.entries_executed   = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.entries_executed);   return; }
    if(counter_name == "entries_blocked")
        { _Diag_Counters.entries_blocked    = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.entries_blocked);    return; }
    if(counter_name == "exits_tp")
        { _Diag_Counters.exits_tp           = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.exits_tp);           return; }
    if(counter_name == "exits_sl")
        { _Diag_Counters.exits_sl           = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.exits_sl);           return; }
    if(counter_name == "exits_manual")
        { _Diag_Counters.exits_manual       = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.exits_manual);       return; }
    if(counter_name == "exits_emergency")
        { _Diag_Counters.exits_emergency    = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.exits_emergency);    return; }
    if(counter_name == "recovery_activated")
        { _Diag_Counters.recovery_activated = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.recovery_activated); return; }
    if(counter_name == "hedge_activated")
        { _Diag_Counters.hedge_activated    = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.hedge_activated);    return; }
    if(counter_name == "basket_opened")
        { _Diag_Counters.basket_opened      = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.basket_opened);      return; }
    if(counter_name == "timer_cycles")
        { _Diag_Counters.timer_cycles       = _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.timer_cycles);       return; }

    // Unknown counter — log and ignore
    Log_Write("DIAG", "WARN",
        "Diag_Increment: unknown counter '" + counter_name + "' — ignored");
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Diag_SetFlag
// Sets a named runtime flag. Logs change at DEBUG level.
// Unknown names log WARN and return — no crash.
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void Diag_SetFlag(string flag_name, bool value)
{
    if(!_Diag_Ready) return;

    string val_str = value ? "true" : "false";

    if(flag_name == "trading_locked")
        { _Diag_Flags.trading_locked  = value; }
    else if(flag_name == "cooldown_active")
        { _Diag_Flags.cooldown_active = value; }
    else if(flag_name == "recovery_active")
        { _Diag_Flags.recovery_active = value; }
    else if(flag_name == "hedge_active")
        { _Diag_Flags.hedge_active    = value; }
    else if(flag_name == "basket_active")
        { _Diag_Flags.basket_active   = value; }
    else if(flag_name == "spread_too_wide")
        { _Diag_Flags.spread_too_wide = value; }
    else if(flag_name == "dd_limit_active")
        { _Diag_Flags.dd_limit_active = value; }
    else
    {
        Log_Write("DIAG", "WARN",
            "Diag_SetFlag: unknown flag '" + flag_name + "' — ignored");
        return;
    }

    Log_Write("DIAG", "DEBUG",
        "Diag_SetFlag  " + flag_name + "=" + val_str);
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Diag_RecordBlock
// Records a blocked entry event.
// Increments: entries_blocked + matching block_* counter.
// Logs block reason at INFO level.
// ARCH REVIEW: renamed from Diag_LogBlock() — unambiguous intent.
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void Diag_RecordBlock(int block_reason)
{
    if(!_Diag_Ready) return;

    // Always increment global blocked counter
    _Diag_Counters.entries_blocked =
        _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.entries_blocked);

    // Increment specific block counter
    switch(block_reason)
    {
        case BLOCK_SPREAD:
            _Diag_Counters.block_spread =
                _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.block_spread); break;
        case BLOCK_DD:
            _Diag_Counters.block_dd =
                _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.block_dd); break;
        case BLOCK_MAX_POS:
            _Diag_Counters.block_max_pos =
                _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.block_max_pos); break;
        case BLOCK_COOLDOWN:
            _Diag_Counters.block_cooldown =
                _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.block_cooldown); break;
        case BLOCK_RISK_HALT:
            _Diag_Counters.block_risk_halt =
                _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.block_risk_halt); break;
        case BLOCK_NO_SIGNAL:
            _Diag_Counters.block_no_signal =
                _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.block_no_signal); break;
        case BLOCK_INVALID_LOT:
            _Diag_Counters.block_invalid_lot =
                _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.block_invalid_lot); break;
        case BLOCK_OTHER:
        default:
            _Diag_Counters.block_other =
                _Diag_SafeIncrement(_Diag_Counters.block_other); break;
    }

    Log_Write("DIAG", "INFO",
        "BLOCK  reason=" + _Diag_BlockName(block_reason) +
        "  total_blocked=" + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.entries_blocked));
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Diag_ExportSummary
// Writes CSV + TXT to MQL5\Files\ — on-demand only.
// ARCH REVIEW: on-demand call, never per-tick.
// Safe to call multiple times — overwrites previous export.
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void Diag_ExportSummary()
{
    if(!_Diag_Ready) return;

    string base     = _Diag_BaseFileName();
    string csv_file = base + ".csv";
    string txt_file = base + ".txt";
    datetime now    = TimeCurrent();
    string now_str  = TimeToString(now, TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS);

    // ── CSV export ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    int csv_h = FileOpen(csv_file,
                         FILE_WRITE | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_SHARE_READ);
    if(csv_h != INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "field,value\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "ea_name,"           + _Diag_EAName                                       + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "symbol,"            + _Diag_Symbol                                       + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "export_time,"       + now_str                                             + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "session_start,"     + TimeToString(_Diag_InitTime,TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "tester,"            + IntegerToString(MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER))         + "\n");
        // Counters
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "signals_buy,"        + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.signals_buy)        + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "signals_sell,"       + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.signals_sell)       + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "signals_none,"       + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.signals_none)       + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "entries_attempted,"  + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.entries_attempted)  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "entries_executed,"   + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.entries_executed)   + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "entries_blocked,"    + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.entries_blocked)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "block_spread,"       + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_spread)       + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "block_dd,"           + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_dd)           + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "block_max_pos,"      + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_max_pos)      + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "block_cooldown,"     + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_cooldown)     + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "block_risk_halt,"    + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_risk_halt)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "block_no_signal,"    + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_no_signal)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "block_invalid_lot,"  + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_invalid_lot)  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "block_other,"        + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_other)        + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "exits_tp,"           + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_tp)           + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "exits_sl,"           + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_sl)           + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "exits_manual,"       + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_manual)       + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "exits_emergency,"    + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_emergency)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "recovery_activated," + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.recovery_activated) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "hedge_activated,"    + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.hedge_activated)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "basket_opened,"      + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.basket_opened)      + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "timer_cycles,"       + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.timer_cycles)       + "\n");
        // Flags
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "flag_trading_locked,"  + IntegerToString(_Diag_Flags.trading_locked)  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "flag_cooldown_active," + IntegerToString(_Diag_Flags.cooldown_active)  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "flag_recovery_active," + IntegerToString(_Diag_Flags.recovery_active)  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "flag_hedge_active,"    + IntegerToString(_Diag_Flags.hedge_active)     + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "flag_basket_active,"   + IntegerToString(_Diag_Flags.basket_active)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "flag_spread_too_wide," + IntegerToString(_Diag_Flags.spread_too_wide)  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(csv_h, "flag_dd_limit_active," + IntegerToString(_Diag_Flags.dd_limit_active)  + "\n");

        FileFlush(csv_h);
        FileClose(csv_h);
        Log_Write("DIAG", "INFO", "ExportSummary CSV  file=" + csv_file);
    }
    else
    {
        Log_Write("DIAG", "ERROR",
            "ExportSummary CSV failed  file=" + csv_file +
            "  err=" + IntegerToString(GetLastError()));
    }

    // ── TXT human-readable export ──────────────────────────────────
    int txt_h = FileOpen(txt_file,
                         FILE_WRITE | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_SHARE_READ);
    if(txt_h != INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        string sep = "────────────────────────────────────────\n";
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "EA_PLATFORM · Diagnostics Summary\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "EA      : " + _Diag_EAName  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "Symbol  : " + _Diag_Symbol  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "Session : " + TimeToString(_Diag_InitTime,TIME_DATE|TIME_SECONDS) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "Export  : " + now_str        + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "Tester  : " + (MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER) ? "YES" : "NO") + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "SIGNALS\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  BUY        : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.signals_buy)  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  SELL       : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.signals_sell) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  NONE       : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.signals_none) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "ENTRIES\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Attempted  : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.entries_attempted) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Executed   : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.entries_executed)  + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Blocked    : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.entries_blocked)   + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "BLOCK REASONS\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Spread     : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_spread)      + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  DD Limit   : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_dd)          + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Max Pos    : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_max_pos)     + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Cooldown   : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_cooldown)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Risk Halt  : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_risk_halt)   + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  No Signal  : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_no_signal)   + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Invalid Lot: " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_invalid_lot) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Other      : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.block_other)       + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "EXITS\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  TP         : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_tp)        + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  SL         : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_sl)        + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Manual     : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_manual)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Emergency  : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_emergency) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "OPTIONAL MODULES\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Recovery   : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.recovery_activated) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Hedge      : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.hedge_activated)    + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  Basket     : " + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.basket_opened)      + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "RUNTIME FLAGS\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  trading_locked  : " + (_Diag_Flags.trading_locked  ? "TRUE" : "false") + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  cooldown_active : " + (_Diag_Flags.cooldown_active ? "TRUE" : "false") + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  recovery_active : " + (_Diag_Flags.recovery_active ? "TRUE" : "false") + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  hedge_active    : " + (_Diag_Flags.hedge_active    ? "TRUE" : "false") + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  basket_active   : " + (_Diag_Flags.basket_active   ? "TRUE" : "false") + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  spread_too_wide : " + (_Diag_Flags.spread_too_wide ? "TRUE" : "false") + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "  dd_limit_active : " + (_Diag_Flags.dd_limit_active ? "TRUE" : "false") + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);
        FileWriteString(txt_h, "CYCLES  timer=" + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.timer_cycles) + "\n");
        FileWriteString(txt_h, sep);

        FileFlush(txt_h);
        FileClose(txt_h);
        Log_Write("DIAG", "INFO", "ExportSummary TXT  file=" + txt_file);
    }
    else
    {
        Log_Write("DIAG", "ERROR",
            "ExportSummary TXT failed  file=" + txt_file +
            "  err=" + IntegerToString(GetLastError()));
    }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Diag_Close
// Called once in OnDeinit(). Exports summary then resets state.
// Safe to call multiple times (idempotent).
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void Diag_Close()
{
    if(!_Diag_Ready) return;

    Log_Write("DIAG", "INFO",
        "Diag_Close  cycles=" + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.timer_cycles) +
        "  entries_blocked="  + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.entries_blocked) +
        "  exits_tp="         + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_tp) +
        "  exits_sl="         + IntegerToString(_Diag_Counters.exits_sl));

    Diag_ExportSummary();

    _Diag_Ready = false;
    _Diag_ZeroCounters();
    _Diag_ZeroFlags();
    _Diag_EAName  = "";
    _Diag_Symbol  = "";
    _Diag_DateTag = "";
}

#endif // __DIAGNOSTICS_V1__
```

---

## FILE 3 — `CORE/Diagnostics_v1_Test.mq5`

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Diagnostics_v1_Test.mq5                                          |
//| EA_PLATFORM · Phase 2 · Unit Test EA                             |
//| Purpose  : Validate Diagnostics_v1.mqh — no trading              |
//| Run in   : Strategy Tester, single pass, any symbol, 1 day       |
//| Expected : All assertions PASS, CSV+TXT files created            |
//| Date     : 2026.06.06                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property strict

#include "Logging_v1.mqh"
#include "Diagnostics_v1.mqh"

input string inp_EA_Name = "DiagTest";

//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Assertion framework
//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
int _t_pass = 0;
int _t_fail = 0;

void _Assert(string id, bool condition, string detail = "")
{
    string msg = (detail == "") ? id : id + "  [" + detail + "]";
    if(condition)
    {
        _t_pass++;
        Print("[PASS] ", msg);
        Log_Write("TEST", "INFO", "[PASS] " + msg);
    }
    else
    {
        _t_fail++;
        Print("[FAIL] ", msg);
        Log_Write("TEST", "ERROR", "[FAIL] " + msg);
    }
}

void _Section(string name)
{
    Print("── ", name, " ──────────────────────────────");
    Log_Write("TEST", "INFO", "── " + name + " ──");
}

//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// OnInit — full test suite
//════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
int OnInit()
{
    // Init logging first (Phase 1 dependency)
    Log_Init(inp_EA_Name, Symbol());

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T01 — Init");
    Diag_Init(inp_EA_Name, Symbol());
    _Assert("T01_Ready",       _Diag_Ready    == true);
    _Assert("T01_EA_Name",     _Diag_EAName   == inp_EA_Name);
    _Assert("T01_Symbol",      _Diag_Symbol   == Symbol());
    _Assert("T01_DateTag_len", StringLen(_Diag_DateTag) == 8);  // YYYYMMDD

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T02 — Counters zero after Init");
    _Assert("T02_signals_buy",       _Diag_Counters.signals_buy       == 0);
    _Assert("T02_entries_attempted", _Diag_Counters.entries_attempted == 0);
    _Assert("T02_entries_blocked",   _Diag_Counters.entries_blocked   == 0);
    _Assert("T02_timer_cycles",      _Diag_Counters.timer_cycles      == 0);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T03 — Flags false after Init");
    _Assert("T03_trading_locked",  _Diag_Flags.trading_locked  == false);
    _Assert("T03_cooldown_active", _Diag_Flags.cooldown_active == false);
    _Assert("T03_recovery_active", _Diag_Flags.recovery_active == false);
    _Assert("T03_dd_limit_active", _Diag_Flags.dd_limit_active == false);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T04 — Diag_Increment known counters");
    Diag_Increment("signals_buy");
    Diag_Increment("signals_buy");
    Diag_Increment("signals_sell");
    Diag_Increment("entries_attempted");
    Diag_Increment("entries_executed");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    _Assert("T04_signals_buy_2",       _Diag_Counters.signals_buy       == 2);
    _Assert("T04_signals_sell_1",      _Diag_Counters.signals_sell      == 1);
    _Assert("T04_entries_attempted_1", _Diag_Counters.entries_attempted == 1);
    _Assert("T04_entries_executed_1",  _Diag_Counters.entries_executed  == 1);
    _Assert("T04_timer_cycles_3",      _Diag_Counters.timer_cycles      == 3);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T05 — Diag_Increment unknown counter");
    // Must NOT crash — WARN logged, counter state unchanged
    int before = _Diag_Counters.signals_buy;
    Diag_Increment("nonexistent_counter_xyz");
    _Assert("T05_no_crash",           true);
    _Assert("T05_state_unchanged",    _Diag_Counters.signals_buy == before);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T06 — Diag_SetFlag known flags");
    Diag_SetFlag("trading_locked",  true);
    Diag_SetFlag("cooldown_active", true);
    Diag_SetFlag("dd_limit_active", true);
    _Assert("T06_trading_locked_true",  _Diag_Flags.trading_locked  == true);
    _Assert("T06_cooldown_active_true", _Diag_Flags.cooldown_active == true);
    _Assert("T06_dd_limit_active_true", _Diag_Flags.dd_limit_active == true);

    Diag_SetFlag("trading_locked",  false);
    _Assert("T06_trading_locked_false", _Diag_Flags.trading_locked == false);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T07 — Diag_SetFlag unknown flag");
    Diag_SetFlag("nonexistent_flag_xyz", true);
    _Assert("T07_no_crash", true);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T08 — Diag_RecordBlock all reasons");
    int blocked_before = _Diag_Counters.entries_blocked;

    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_SPREAD);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_DD);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_MAX_POS);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_COOLDOWN);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_RISK_HALT);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_NO_SIGNAL);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_INVALID_LOT);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_OTHER);

    _Assert("T08_entries_blocked_plus8",
            _Diag_Counters.entries_blocked == blocked_before + 8);
    _Assert("T08_block_spread_1",       _Diag_Counters.block_spread      == 1);
    _Assert("T08_block_dd_1",           _Diag_Counters.block_dd          == 1);
    _Assert("T08_block_max_pos_1",      _Diag_Counters.block_max_pos     == 1);
    _Assert("T08_block_cooldown_1",     _Diag_Counters.block_cooldown    == 1);
    _Assert("T08_block_risk_halt_1",    _Diag_Counters.block_risk_halt   == 1);
    _Assert("T08_block_no_signal_1",    _Diag_Counters.block_no_signal   == 1);
    _Assert("T08_block_invalid_lot_1",  _Diag_Counters.block_invalid_lot == 1);
    _Assert("T08_block_other_1",        _Diag_Counters.block_other       == 1);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T09 — Diag_RecordBlock unknown enum value (out-of-range)");
    int other_before = _Diag_Counters.block_other;
    Diag_RecordBlock(999);  // must fall to default: block_other
    _Assert("T09_no_crash",    true);
    _Assert("T09_block_other", _Diag_Counters.block_other == other_before + 1);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T10 — ExportSummary creates CSV and TXT");
    Diag_ExportSummary();
    string base     = _Diag_BaseFileName();
    string csv_file = base + ".csv";
    string txt_file = base + ".txt";

    int csv_h = FileOpen(csv_file, FILE_READ | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_SHARE_READ);
    bool csv_ok = (csv_h != INVALID_HANDLE);
    if(csv_ok) FileClose(csv_h);
    _Assert("T10_CSV_created", csv_ok, csv_file);

    int txt_h = FileOpen(txt_file, FILE_READ | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_SHARE_READ);
    bool txt_ok = (txt_h != INVALID_HANDLE);
    if(txt_ok) FileClose(txt_h);
    _Assert("T10_TXT_created", txt_ok, txt_file);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T11 — Diag_Reset zeroes state, log stays open");
    Diag_Reset();
    _Assert("T11_Ready_after_reset",     _Diag_Ready                      == true);
    _Assert("T11_signals_buy_zero",      _Diag_Counters.signals_buy       == 0);
    _Assert("T11_entries_blocked_zero",  _Diag_Counters.entries_blocked   == 0);
    _Assert("T11_trading_locked_false",  _Diag_Flags.trading_locked       == false);
    _Assert("T11_log_still_works",       true);  // Log_Write after reset
    Log_Write("TEST", "INFO", "T11 post-reset log write OK");

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T12 — Zero-trade scenario (no increments after reset)");
    // Simulate: EA ran but signal never fired
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    Diag_Increment("signals_none");
    _Assert("T12_timer_2",       _Diag_Counters.timer_cycles  == 2);
    _Assert("T12_signals_none_1",_Diag_Counters.signals_none  == 1);
    _Assert("T12_entries_zero",  _Diag_Counters.entries_attempted == 0);
    Diag_ExportSummary();  // export with zero entries — must not crash
    _Assert("T12_export_ok",     true);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T13 — Diag_Close then idempotent second close");
    Diag_Close();
    _Assert("T13_Ready_false_after_close", _Diag_Ready == false);

    Diag_Close();  // second call — must not crash
    _Assert("T13_Idempotent_close",        true);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T14 — Diag_Increment after close (no crash)");
    Diag_Increment("signals_buy");  // _Diag_Ready=false → returns immediately
    _Assert("T14_no_crash_after_close",   true);
    _Assert("T14_counter_unchanged",      _Diag_Counters.signals_buy == 0);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T15 — Repeated Init (re-attach simulation)");
    Diag_Init(inp_EA_Name + "_v2", Symbol());
    _Assert("T15_Ready_after_reinit",  _Diag_Ready == true);
    _Assert("T15_New_EAName",          _Diag_EAName == inp_EA_Name + "_v2");
    _Assert("T15_Counters_reset",      _Diag_Counters.signals_buy == 0);
    Diag_Increment("signals_sell");
    _Assert("T15_Increment_after_reinit", _Diag_Counters.signals_sell == 1);
    Diag_Close();

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Final summary
    Print("════════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("Diagnostics_v1 Test Results");
    Print("  PASS  : ", _t_pass);
    Print("  FAIL  : ", _t_fail);
    Print("  TOTAL : ", _t_pass + _t_fail);
    Print("════════════════════════════════════════");
    Print(_t_fail == 0
          ? "[RESULT] ALL PASS — Diagnostics_v1 Phase 2 complete"
          : "[RESULT] FAILURES DETECTED — review above");

    Log_Close();
    return(_t_fail == 0 ? INIT_SUCCEEDED : INIT_FAILED);
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
    Diag_Close();
    Log_Close();
}

void OnTick() { }  // no trading
```

---

## Known Edge Cases

| Case | Risk | Status |
|------|------|--------|
| `static` vars in `.mqh` | State collision risk in Phase 3+ complex include chains | Accepted Phase 2; document for Phase 3 migration to `g_Diag` struct |
| CSV file open fails in Tester sandbox | File path permissions vary by broker/terminal install | T10 explicitly verifies file existence; WARN logged on failure |
| `Diag_RecordBlock(999)` out-of-range | `switch` default catches all unknown values → `block_other` | Covered by T09 |
| `Diag_ExportSummary()` called before `Diag_Init()` | `_Diag_Ready = false` guard returns immediately | Safe |
| Long session counter overflow | `_Diag_SafeIncrement()` caps at `INT_MAX`, no wrap | Covered by design |
| `Diag_Close()` called from `OnDeinit()` after crash | File handles may already be closed by terminal | Idempotent guard on `_Diag_Ready` prevents double-close |
| EA re-attached mid-session | `Diag_Init()` resets all state cleanly | Covered by T15 |

---

## Troubleshooting Guide

| Symptom | Likely Cause | Fix |
|---------|-------------|-----|
| C2 compile error: `Log_Write undeclared` | `Logging_v1.mqh` not in same folder or wrong path | Verify CORE/ folder contains all Phase 1 files |
| C2 compile error: `INVALID_HANDLE undeclared` | Missing `#property strict` or old MetaEditor | Add `#property strict` to top of test EA |
| T10 FAIL: CSV not created | Terminal MQL5\Files\ sandbox restriction | Check terminal file permissions; try without `FILE_SHARE_READ` flag |
| T10 FAIL: TXT not created | Same as above | Same fix |
| Log file not appearing | `Log_Init()` not called before `Diag_Init()` in test | Confirm call order in OnInit() |
| All FAIL — `_Diag_Ready` always false | `Diag_Init()` not reached before assertions | Check OnInit() order — Log_Init must come first |
| `[WARN] unknown counter` in log for valid name | Typo in counter string literal | Compare exactly against `DiagCounters` field names in contract |
| Export file from previous run mixed with new run | Same date tag on same day re-run | Expected behavior — file overwrites; verify timestamp inside file |
