## PATCH — `Diagnostics_v1.mqh` — Getter Functions Added

เพิ่มต่อจาก `Diag_Close()` ก่อน `#endif` ครับ:

```mql5
//====================================================================
// PUBLIC READ-ONLY GETTERS
// Contract: Diagnostics_Contract.mqh §Getter Functions
// - Never modify internal state
// - Unknown names: return 0/false, log WARN only if _Diag_Ready
// - Never crash
//====================================================================

bool Diag_IsReady()
{
    return _Diag_Ready;
}

string Diag_GetEAName()
{
    return _Diag_EAName;
}

string Diag_GetSymbol()
{
    return _Diag_Symbol;
}

string Diag_GetDateTag()
{
    return _Diag_DateTag;
}

int Diag_GetCounter(string counter_name)
{
    if(counter_name == "signals_buy")        return _Diag_Counters.signals_buy;
    if(counter_name == "signals_sell")       return _Diag_Counters.signals_sell;
    if(counter_name == "signals_none")       return _Diag_Counters.signals_none;
    if(counter_name == "entries_attempted")  return _Diag_Counters.entries_attempted;
    if(counter_name == "entries_executed")   return _Diag_Counters.entries_executed;
    if(counter_name == "entries_blocked")    return _Diag_Counters.entries_blocked;
    if(counter_name == "block_spread")       return _Diag_Counters.block_spread;
    if(counter_name == "block_dd")           return _Diag_Counters.block_dd;
    if(counter_name == "block_max_pos")      return _Diag_Counters.block_max_pos;
    if(counter_name == "block_cooldown")     return _Diag_Counters.block_cooldown;
    if(counter_name == "block_risk_halt")    return _Diag_Counters.block_risk_halt;
    if(counter_name == "block_no_signal")    return _Diag_Counters.block_no_signal;
    if(counter_name == "block_invalid_lot")  return _Diag_Counters.block_invalid_lot;
    if(counter_name == "block_other")        return _Diag_Counters.block_other;
    if(counter_name == "exits_tp")           return _Diag_Counters.exits_tp;
    if(counter_name == "exits_sl")           return _Diag_Counters.exits_sl;
    if(counter_name == "exits_manual")       return _Diag_Counters.exits_manual;
    if(counter_name == "exits_emergency")    return _Diag_Counters.exits_emergency;
    if(counter_name == "recovery_activated") return _Diag_Counters.recovery_activated;
    if(counter_name == "hedge_activated")    return _Diag_Counters.hedge_activated;
    if(counter_name == "basket_opened")      return _Diag_Counters.basket_opened;
    if(counter_name == "timer_cycles")       return _Diag_Counters.timer_cycles;

    // Unknown — log WARN only when ready, never crash
    if(_Diag_Ready)
        Log_Write("DIAG", "WARN",
            "Diag_GetCounter: unknown counter '" + counter_name + "' — returned 0");
    return 0;
}

bool Diag_GetFlag(string flag_name)
{
    if(flag_name == "trading_locked")  return _Diag_Flags.trading_locked;
    if(flag_name == "cooldown_active") return _Diag_Flags.cooldown_active;
    if(flag_name == "recovery_active") return _Diag_Flags.recovery_active;
    if(flag_name == "hedge_active")    return _Diag_Flags.hedge_active;
    if(flag_name == "basket_active")   return _Diag_Flags.basket_active;
    if(flag_name == "spread_too_wide") return _Diag_Flags.spread_too_wide;
    if(flag_name == "dd_limit_active") return _Diag_Flags.dd_limit_active;

    // Unknown — log WARN only when ready, never crash
    if(_Diag_Ready)
        Log_Write("DIAG", "WARN",
            "Diag_GetFlag: unknown flag '" + flag_name + "' — returned false");
    return false;
}
```

---

## PATCH — `Diagnostics_Contract.mqh` — Getter Section Added

เพิ่มหลัง `## Diag_Close` section:

```markdown
## Getter Functions — Read-Only Public API

All getters are read-only. They never modify internal state.
Unknown names: return 0 or false. Log WARN only when _Diag_Ready is true. Never crash.
Getters work correctly before and after Diag_Close() — return zero-value state silently.

### Diag_IsReady
    bool Diag_IsReady()
Returns true if Diag_Init() has been called and Diag_Close() has not yet been called.
Use to guard external reads. Does not log.

### Diag_GetEAName
    string Diag_GetEAName()
Returns the EA name passed to Diag_Init(). Returns "" if not initialized.

### Diag_GetSymbol
    string Diag_GetSymbol()
Returns the symbol passed to Diag_Init(). Returns "" if not initialized.

### Diag_GetDateTag
    string Diag_GetDateTag()
Returns YYYYMMDD string built at Diag_Init() time. Returns "" if not initialized.

### Diag_GetCounter
    int Diag_GetCounter(string counter_name)
Returns current value of named counter. Returns 0 for unknown name.
counter_name must exactly match a DiagCounters field name.

### Diag_GetFlag
    bool Diag_GetFlag(string flag_name)
Returns current value of named flag. Returns false for unknown name.
flag_name must exactly match a DiagFlags field name.
```

---

## PATCH — `Diagnostics_v1_Test.mq5` — Full Rewrite (internal access removed)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Diagnostics_v1_Test.mq5                                          |
//| EA_PLATFORM · Phase 2 · Unit Test EA · PATCHED                   |
//| Patch    : Remove direct _Diag_* internal access                 |
//|            Use only public Diag_ getter API                      |
//| Purpose  : Validate Diagnostics_v1.mqh — no trading              |
//| Run in   : Strategy Tester, single pass, any symbol, 1 day       |
//| Expected : All assertions PASS, CSV+TXT created                  |
//| Date     : 2026.06.06                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property strict

#include "Logging_v1.mqh"
#include "Diagnostics_v1.mqh"

input string inp_EA_Name = "DiagTest";

//====================================================================
// Assertion framework
//====================================================================
int _t_pass = 0;
int _t_fail = 0;

void _Assert(string id, bool condition, string detail = "")
{
    string msg = (detail == "") ? id : id + "  [" + detail + "]";
    if(condition)
    {
        _t_pass++;
        Print("[PASS] ", msg);
        Log_Write("TEST", "INFO", "[PASS] " + msg);
    }
    else
    {
        _t_fail++;
        Print("[FAIL] ", msg);
        Log_Write("TEST", "ERROR", "[FAIL] " + msg);
    }
}

void _Section(string name)
{
    Print("── ", name, " ──");
    Log_Write("TEST", "INFO", "── " + name + " ──");
}

//====================================================================
// OnInit — full test suite (public API only)
//====================================================================
int OnInit()
{
    Log_Init(inp_EA_Name, Symbol());

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T01 — Init + Diag_IsReady / Getters");
    Diag_Init(inp_EA_Name, Symbol());
    _Assert("T01_IsReady",      Diag_IsReady()     == true);
    _Assert("T01_GetEAName",    Diag_GetEAName()   == inp_EA_Name);
    _Assert("T01_GetSymbol",    Diag_GetSymbol()   == Symbol());
    _Assert("T01_DateTag_len",  StringLen(Diag_GetDateTag()) == 8);  // YYYYMMDD

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T02 — Counters zero after Init via Diag_GetCounter");
    _Assert("T02_signals_buy",       Diag_GetCounter("signals_buy")       == 0);
    _Assert("T02_entries_attempted", Diag_GetCounter("entries_attempted") == 0);
    _Assert("T02_entries_blocked",   Diag_GetCounter("entries_blocked")   == 0);
    _Assert("T02_timer_cycles",      Diag_GetCounter("timer_cycles")      == 0);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T03 — Flags false after Init via Diag_GetFlag");
    _Assert("T03_trading_locked",  Diag_GetFlag("trading_locked")  == false);
    _Assert("T03_cooldown_active", Diag_GetFlag("cooldown_active") == false);
    _Assert("T03_recovery_active", Diag_GetFlag("recovery_active") == false);
    _Assert("T03_dd_limit_active", Diag_GetFlag("dd_limit_active") == false);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T04 — Diag_Increment + Diag_GetCounter verify");
    Diag_Increment("signals_buy");
    Diag_Increment("signals_buy");
    Diag_Increment("signals_sell");
    Diag_Increment("entries_attempted");
    Diag_Increment("entries_executed");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    _Assert("T04_signals_buy_2",       Diag_GetCounter("signals_buy")       == 2);
    _Assert("T04_signals_sell_1",      Diag_GetCounter("signals_sell")      == 1);
    _Assert("T04_entries_attempted_1", Diag_GetCounter("entries_attempted") == 1);
    _Assert("T04_entries_executed_1",  Diag_GetCounter("entries_executed")  == 1);
    _Assert("T04_timer_cycles_3",      Diag_GetCounter("timer_cycles")      == 3);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T05 — Diag_Increment unknown counter");
    int before = Diag_GetCounter("signals_buy");
    Diag_Increment("nonexistent_counter_xyz");
    _Assert("T05_no_crash",        true);
    _Assert("T05_state_unchanged", Diag_GetCounter("signals_buy") == before);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T06 — Diag_SetFlag + Diag_GetFlag verify");
    Diag_SetFlag("trading_locked",  true);
    Diag_SetFlag("cooldown_active", true);
    Diag_SetFlag("dd_limit_active", true);
    _Assert("T06_trading_locked_true",  Diag_GetFlag("trading_locked")  == true);
    _Assert("T06_cooldown_active_true", Diag_GetFlag("cooldown_active") == true);
    _Assert("T06_dd_limit_active_true", Diag_GetFlag("dd_limit_active") == true);
    Diag_SetFlag("trading_locked", false);
    _Assert("T06_trading_locked_false", Diag_GetFlag("trading_locked")  == false);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T07 — Diag_SetFlag unknown flag");
    Diag_SetFlag("nonexistent_flag_xyz", true);
    _Assert("T07_no_crash", true);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T08 — Diag_GetFlag unknown flag returns false + no crash");
    bool unk_flag = Diag_GetFlag("nonexistent_flag_abc");
    _Assert("T08_getter_unknown_flag_no_crash",    true);
    _Assert("T08_getter_unknown_flag_returns_false", unk_flag == false);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T09 — Diag_GetCounter unknown counter returns 0 + no crash");
    int unk_ctr = Diag_GetCounter("nonexistent_counter_abc");
    _Assert("T09_getter_unknown_counter_no_crash",    true);
    _Assert("T09_getter_unknown_counter_returns_zero", unk_ctr == 0);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T10 — Diag_RecordBlock all 8 reasons");
    int blocked_before = Diag_GetCounter("entries_blocked");
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_SPREAD);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_DD);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_MAX_POS);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_COOLDOWN);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_RISK_HALT);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_NO_SIGNAL);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_INVALID_LOT);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_OTHER);
    _Assert("T10_entries_blocked_plus8",
            Diag_GetCounter("entries_blocked")   == blocked_before + 8);
    _Assert("T10_block_spread_1",      Diag_GetCounter("block_spread")      == 1);
    _Assert("T10_block_dd_1",          Diag_GetCounter("block_dd")          == 1);
    _Assert("T10_block_max_pos_1",     Diag_GetCounter("block_max_pos")     == 1);
    _Assert("T10_block_cooldown_1",    Diag_GetCounter("block_cooldown")    == 1);
    _Assert("T10_block_risk_halt_1",   Diag_GetCounter("block_risk_halt")   == 1);
    _Assert("T10_block_no_signal_1",   Diag_GetCounter("block_no_signal")   == 1);
    _Assert("T10_block_invalid_lot_1", Diag_GetCounter("block_invalid_lot") == 1);
    _Assert("T10_block_other_1",       Diag_GetCounter("block_other")       == 1);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T11 — Diag_RecordBlock out-of-range falls to block_other");
    int other_before = Diag_GetCounter("block_other");
    Diag_RecordBlock(999);
    _Assert("T11_no_crash",    true);
    _Assert("T11_block_other", Diag_GetCounter("block_other") == other_before + 1);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T12 — ExportSummary creates CSV and TXT");
    Diag_ExportSummary();
    // Derive base filename using public getters only
    string ea_clean  = Diag_GetEAName();
    string sym_clean = Diag_GetSymbol();
    StringReplace(ea_clean,  " ", "_");
    StringReplace(sym_clean, " ", "_");
    string base     = "diagnostics_" + ea_clean + "_" + sym_clean +
                      "_" + Diag_GetDateTag();
    string csv_file = base + ".csv";
    string txt_file = base + ".txt";

    int csv_h = FileOpen(csv_file,
                    FILE_READ | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_SHARE_READ);
    bool csv_ok = (csv_h != INVALID_HANDLE);
    if(csv_ok) FileClose(csv_h);
    _Assert("T12_CSV_created", csv_ok, csv_file);

    int txt_h = FileOpen(txt_file,
                    FILE_READ | FILE_TXT | FILE_ANSI | FILE_SHARE_READ);
    bool txt_ok = (txt_h != INVALID_HANDLE);
    if(txt_ok) FileClose(txt_h);
    _Assert("T12_TXT_created", txt_ok, txt_file);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T13 — Diag_Reset zeroes state; getters reflect zero");
    Diag_Reset();
    _Assert("T13_IsReady_after_reset",     Diag_IsReady()                        == true);
    _Assert("T13_signals_buy_zero",        Diag_GetCounter("signals_buy")        == 0);
    _Assert("T13_entries_blocked_zero",    Diag_GetCounter("entries_blocked")    == 0);
    _Assert("T13_trading_locked_false",    Diag_GetFlag("trading_locked")        == false);
    _Assert("T13_dd_limit_active_false",   Diag_GetFlag("dd_limit_active")       == false);
    _Assert("T13_EAName_preserved",        Diag_GetEAName()                      == inp_EA_Name);
    Log_Write("TEST", "INFO", "T13 post-reset log write OK");
    _Assert("T13_log_still_works", true);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T14 — Zero-trade scenario export");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    Diag_Increment("timer_cycles");
    Diag_Increment("signals_none");
    _Assert("T14_timer_2",        Diag_GetCounter("timer_cycles")      == 2);
    _Assert("T14_signals_none_1", Diag_GetCounter("signals_none")      == 1);
    _Assert("T14_entries_zero",   Diag_GetCounter("entries_attempted") == 0);
    Diag_ExportSummary();
    _Assert("T14_export_ok", true);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T15 — Diag_Close; IsReady false; getters return safe defaults");
    Diag_Close();
    _Assert("T15_IsReady_false_after_close", Diag_IsReady()   == false);
    // Getters after close must return zero-value, no crash
    _Assert("T15_GetEAName_after_close",     Diag_GetEAName() == "");
    _Assert("T15_GetSymbol_after_close",     Diag_GetSymbol() == "");
    _Assert("T15_GetDateTag_after_close",    Diag_GetDateTag() == "");
    _Assert("T15_GetCounter_after_close",    Diag_GetCounter("signals_buy") == 0);
    _Assert("T15_GetFlag_after_close",       Diag_GetFlag("trading_locked") == false);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T16 — Idempotent double close");
    Diag_Close();
    _Assert("T16_double_close_no_crash", true);
    _Assert("T16_IsReady_still_false",   Diag_IsReady() == false);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T17 — All Diag_ calls after close are no-ops; no crash");
    Diag_Increment("signals_buy");
    _Assert("T17_Increment_no_crash",    true);
    _Assert("T17_counter_still_zero",    Diag_GetCounter("signals_buy") == 0);
    Diag_SetFlag("trading_locked", true);
    _Assert("T17_SetFlag_no_crash",      true);
    _Assert("T17_flag_still_false",      Diag_GetFlag("trading_locked") == false);
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_DD);
    _Assert("T17_RecordBlock_no_crash",  true);
    Diag_ExportSummary();
    _Assert("T17_Export_no_crash",       true);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _Section("T18 — Re-Init re-attach simulation");
    Diag_Init(inp_EA_Name + "_v2", Symbol());
    _Assert("T18_IsReady_after_reinit",  Diag_IsReady()                 == true);
    _Assert("T18_New_EAName",            Diag_GetEAName()               == inp_EA_Name + "_v2");
    _Assert("T18_Symbol_correct",        Diag_GetSymbol()               == Symbol());
    _Assert("T18_Counters_reset",        Diag_GetCounter("signals_buy") == 0);
    Diag_Increment("signals_sell");
    _Assert("T18_Increment_after_reinit", Diag_GetCounter("signals_sell") == 1);
    Diag_Close();
    _Assert("T18_IsReady_false_after_close", Diag_IsReady() == false);

    //────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Final summary
    Print("════════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("Diagnostics_v1 Patch Test Results");
    Print("  PASS  : ", _t_pass);
    Print("  FAIL  : ", _t_fail);
    Print("  TOTAL : ", _t_pass + _t_fail);
    Print("════════════════════════════════════════");
    if(_t_fail == 0)
        Print("[RESULT] ALL PASS — Diagnostics_v1 Phase 2 PATCH complete");
    else
        Print("[RESULT] FAILURES DETECTED — review above");

    Log_Close();
    return(_t_fail == 0 ? INIT_SUCCEEDED : INIT_FAILED);
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason) { Diag_Close(); Log_Close(); }
void OnTick()  { }
```

## Phase 3 — RiskEngine_v1 Architect Review

---

### Context: What Phase 3 inherits

```
Logging_v1      ← stable, Pattern C
Diagnostics_v1  ← stable, Pattern C + getter API
RiskEngine_v1   ← Phase 3, depends on both above
```

---

### Issue 1 — Static Variable Pattern: Migration Decision Required (Critical)

Phase 1 และ 2 ใช้ `static` vars ใน `.mqh` และ document ไว้ว่า Phase 3 ต้อง decide migration

ตอนนี้ถึงจุดนั้นแล้วครับ มีสองทาง:

**Option A — ยังคง `static` pattern (Phase 3 เท่านั้น)**

```mql5
static RiskState _Risk_State;
static bool      _Risk_Ready = false;
```

- Pro: consistent กับ Phase 1–2, เร็ว
- Con: Phase 4+ เริ่ม include chain ซับซ้อน, collision risk เพิ่มขึ้น

**Option B — Migrate เป็น global struct ตั้งแต่ Phase 3**

```mql5
struct RiskState { ... };
RiskState g_Risk;   // declared once in EA_TEMPLATE.mq5
```

- Pro: ownership ชัด, no collision, future-safe, Phase 3 เป็น turning point ที่ดี
- Con: ต้องเปลี่ยน pattern จาก Phase 1–2

**Recommendation: Option A ก่อน** — เหตุผลคือ EA_TEMPLATE skeleton (Phase 4) ยังไม่มี ถ้า migrate เป็น global struct ตอนนี้ต้องสร้าง `g_Risk` ใน EA_TEMPLATE ที่ยังไม่ exist → chicken-and-egg problem ทำให้ test EA ซับซ้อนขึ้นโดยไม่จำเป็น

> **Migrate เป็น global struct ที่ Phase 4 (EA_TEMPLATE skeleton)** — ตอนนั้น EA_TEMPLATE จะเป็น owner ของ `g_Risk`, `g_Diag` ทั้งหมดในที่เดียว

---

### Issue 2 — Tick Value Calculation: Symbol-Specific Risk (Critical)

Lot size formula standard:

```
lot = (account_balance * risk_pct / 100) / (sl_points * tick_value)
```

ปัญหาคือ `tick_value` ต่างกันมากระหว่าง symbols:

| Symbol | Contract size | Tick size | Tick value (approx USD) |
|--------|--------------|-----------|------------------------|
| EURUSD | 100,000 | 0.00001 | $1.00 per pip |
| XAUUSD | 100 oz | 0.01 | $1.00 per tick |
| BTCUSD | 1 BTC | 0.01 | ~$0.01 per tick (varies) |

**ห้าม hardcode** — ต้องใช้ `SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE)` จาก broker เท่านั้น เพราะ broker แต่ละเจ้า define contract size ต่างกัน

**Additional risk:** ถ้า account currency ไม่ใช่ USD (เช่น THB, EUR) — `tick_value` ที่ broker return อาจเป็น account currency แล้ว หรืออาจต้องแปลงเอง ต้อง handle ทั้งสองกรณี

**Recommendation:** ใช้ `SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE` เป็น primary source + เพิ่ม sanity check ว่า tick_value > 0 ก่อน calculate ทุกครั้ง — ถ้า 0 หรือ invalid → halt + log ERROR

---

### Issue 3 — Daily DD Reset: Server Time vs Local Time (Moderate)

Daily DD reset ต้องใช้ **server time** (`TimeCurrent()`) ไม่ใช่ local time (`TimeLocal()`) เพราะ:
- Broker session rollover ใช้ server time
- Strategy Tester ใช้ historical server time
- Local time อาจต่างกับ server หลายชั่วโมง (timezone)

**Reset trigger design:** เปรียบเทียบ `day-of-year` ระหว่าง last reset timestamp กับ `TimeCurrent()` — ถ้าต่างกัน → reset daily DD counter

**Edge case:** EA รันข้าม midnight ต้องไม่ miss reset — timer cycle ต้องเช็คทุก cycle ไม่ใช่เฉพาะตอน trade

---

### Issue 4 — RiskResult Struct: Separation of Concerns (Design Decision)

`RiskResult` ถูก return จาก `Risk_CalculateLot()` — ต้อง decide ว่าจะใส่อะไรใน struct

**Recommended minimal struct:**

```mql5
struct RiskResult {
    double  lot_size;       // calculated lot (0.0 = rejected)
    bool    trade_allowed;  // false = do not trade
    string  halt_reason;    // populated when trade_allowed = false
    double  daily_dd_pct;   // snapshot at calculation time
    double  total_dd_pct;   // snapshot at calculation time
};
```

**ไม่ใส่:** SL price, TP price, signal data — นั่นเป็น responsibility ของ Signal และ Entry Engine ไม่ใช่ Risk Engine separation of concerns ชัด

---

### Issue 5 — Halt State Persistence (Moderate)

ถ้า Risk Engine halt เพราะ DD limit — halt state ต้องอยู่ข้ามหลาย timer cycles จนกว่าจะ reset

**สองกรณีที่ต้อง handle:**
- **Daily DD halt** → reset อัตโนมัติเมื่อขึ้น trading day ใหม่ (server time)
- **Total DD halt** → ไม่ reset อัตโนมัติ → ต้อง manual reset (human decision)

**Recommendation:** แยก flag สองตัว: `daily_halt` (auto-reset) และ `total_halt` (manual-only)

---

### Issue 6 — Lot Normalization (Minor but Critical in Live)

Broker กำหนด:
- `SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN` — minimum lot
- `SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX` — maximum lot  
- `SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP` — lot step (e.g. 0.01)

Calculated lot ต้อง normalize ให้ตรงกับ `VOLUME_STEP` ก่อน return เสมอ มิฉะนั้น `OrderSend()` จะ reject ด้วย `TRADE_RETCODE_INVALID_VOLUME`

```mql5
double step = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
lot = MathFloor(lot / step) * step;  // round DOWN always
lot = MathMax(lot, SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
lot = MathMin(lot, inp_Risk_MaxLotSize);
```

**Round DOWN เสมอ** — ห้าม round up เพราะจะทำให้ risk เกิน policy

---

### Summary: All Issues

| # | Issue | Severity | Decision |
|---|-------|----------|----------|
| 1 | Static var pattern migration | Critical | **Defer to Phase 4** — remain static in Phase 3 |
| 2 | Tick value: symbol-specific, broker-specific | Critical | Use `SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE` + sanity check; halt if 0 |
| 3 | Daily DD reset: server time | Moderate | Use `TimeCurrent()` only; day-of-year comparison per cycle |
| 4 | RiskResult struct scope | Design | Minimal struct — lot, allowed, reason, dd snapshots only |
| 5 | Halt state persistence | Moderate | Two flags: `daily_halt` (auto-reset) + `total_halt` (manual) |
| 6 | Lot normalization | Minor/Critical | `MathFloor` to `VOLUME_STEP`, clamp to min/max, always round down |

---

ทั้ง 6 issues จะถูก apply เข้าไปใน implementation โดยตรงครับ

**Approve build ได้เลย หรือถ้าอยากปรับ decision ใดก่อน บอกได้ครับ**

Add Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics().  
  
It must validate:  
- SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE > 0  
- SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE > 0  
- SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN > 0  
- SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP > 0

- set risk halt  
- set halt_reason = SYMBOL_ECONOMICS_FAIL  
- Log_Write("RISK","ERROR", ...)  
- Diag_SetFlag("trading_locked", true)  
- return false  
  
Patch 8 — Manual Reset  
Add Risk_ManualReset().  
  
It may reset:  
- total_halt  
- manual_halt  
- symbol_economics_fail  
- halt_reason  
  
It must NOT reset:  
- daily DD baseline incorrectly  
- account balance history silently  
- active DD state while current DD is still beyond limit  
  
Daily halt auto-resets only on new server day.  
Total halt resets manual only.

tick_size.\\n- Returns RiskResult with trade_allowed = false if:\\n  - Any halt flag active\\n  - sl_distance_points &lt;= 0\\n  - tick_value &lt;= 0 (symbol economics invalid)\\n  - Calculated raw lot &lt;= 0\\n- Normalizes lot: MathFloor to SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP (round DOWN always).\\n- Clamps lot: max(SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN, min(calculated, inp_Risk_MaxLotSize)).\\n- Populates daily_dd_pct and calculation.\\n\\n## Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics\\n\\n```\\nbool Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics(string symbol)\\n```\\n\\n- Validates: SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE &gt; 0\\n- Validates: SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE &gt; 0\\n- Validates: SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN &gt; 0\\n- Validates: SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP &gt; 0\\n- Validates: OrderCalcProfit() returns non-zero for a small test movement (1 lot, 10 point move).\\n- On any failure: sets symbol_economics_fail = true, halt_reason = RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON, calls Diag_SetFlag(\\\&quot;trading_locked\\\&quot;, true), logs ERROR.\\n- Returns true only if ALL validations symbol_economics_fail, halt_reason (cleared only when all three flags become false).\\n- MUST NOT reset: daily_dd_pct, daily_start_balance, session_high_balance, account_balance, last_reset_day.\\n- MUST NOT reset daily_halt \\u2014 daily_halt auto-resets on new server day only.\\n- Safety check: if current total_dd_pct still exceeds inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct, total_halt reset is blocked and WARN is   // Max risk per trade (%)\\ninput double inp_Risk_MaxDailyDDPct = 5.0;   // Max daily DD (%)\\ninput double inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct = 15.0;  // Max total DD (%)\\ninput double inp_Risk_MaxLotSize    = 10.0;  // Hard lot ceiling\\ninput double inp_Risk_MinLotSize    = 0.01;  // Hard lot floor\\n#endif\\n\\n//====================================================================\\n// INTERNAL STATE\\n// D1: static vars acceptable for Phase 3 standalone.\\n// Phase 4 migration: declare as RiskState g_Risk in EA_TEMPLATE.mq5\\n//====================================================================\\nstatic RiskState _Risk_State;\\nstatic string    _Risk_Symbol = \\\&quot;\\\&quot;;\\nstatic bool      _Risk_Ready  = false;\\n\\n//====================================================================\\n// INTERNAL HELPERS\\n//====================================================================\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Get day-of-year from datetime (1..366)\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nstatic int _Risk_GetDayOfYear(datetime dt)\\n{\\n    MqlDateTime s;\\n    TimeToStruct(dt, s);\\n    return s.day_of_year;\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Normalize lot to broker volume step \\u2014 round DOWN always (D6)\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nstatic double _Risk_NormalizeLot(double raw_lot, string symbol)\\n{\\n    double step = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);\\n    double vmin = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);\\n    double vmax = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);\\n\\n    if(step &lt;= 0.0) step = 0.01;   // fallback; should not happen post-sanity-check\\n\\n    double lot = MathFloor(raw_lot / step) * step;  // round DOWN\\n    lot = MathMax(lot, vmin);\\n    lot = MathMax(lot, inp_Risk_MinLotSize);\\n    lot = MathMin(lot, inp_Risk_MaxLotSize);\\n    lot = MathMin(lot, vmax);\\n\\n    // Final normalize to broker precision\\n    lot = NormalizeDouble(lot, 2);\\n    return lot;\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Zero all RiskState fields\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nstatic void _Risk_ZeroState()\\n{\\n    _Risk_State.account_balance       = 0.0;\\n    _Risk_State.session_high_balance  = 0.0;\\n    _Risk_State.daily_start_balance   = 0.0;\\n    _Risk_State.daily_dd_pct          = 0.0;\\n    _Risk_State.total_dd_pct          = 0.0;\\n    _Risk_State.daily_halt            = false;\\n    _Risk_State.total_halt            = false;\\n    _Risk_State.manual_halt           = false;\\n    _Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail = false;\\n    _Risk_State.last_reset_day        = 0;\\n    _Risk_State.halt_reason           = \\\&quot;\\\&quot;;\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Set halt: flag + reason + Diagnostics flag + log\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nstatic void _Risk_SetHalt(string reason)\\n{\\n    _Risk_State.halt_reason = reason;\\n\\n    if(reason == RISK_HALT_DAILY_DD)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_State.daily_halt = true;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;HALT SET  reason=\\\&quot; + reason +\\n            \\\&quot;  daily_dd=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.daily_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot;);\\n    }\\n    else if(reason == RISK_HALT_TOTAL_DD)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_State.total_halt = true;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;HALT SET  reason=\\\&quot; + reason +\\n            \\\&quot;  total_dd=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot;);\\n    }\\n    else if(reason == RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail = true;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;HALT SET  reason=\\\&quot; + reason);\\n    }\\n    else\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;HALT SET  reason=\\\&quot; + reason);\\n    }\\n\\n    Diag_SetFlag(\\\&quot;trading_locked\\\&quot;, true);\\n    Diag_SetFlag(\\\&quot;dd_limit_active\\\&quot;,\\n        _Risk_State.daily_halt || _Risk_State.total_halt);\\n}\\n\\n//====================================================================\\n// PUBLIC API\\n//====================================================================\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics \\u2014 Patch 7\\n// Validates broker symbol parameters before any lot calculation.\\n// Sets halt and returns false if any check fails.\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nbool Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics(string symbol)\\n{\\n    bool ok = true;\\n\\n    double tick_value = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);\\n    double tick_size  = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);\\n    double vol_min    = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);\\n    double vol_step   = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);\\n\\n    if(tick_value &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CheckSymbolEconomics FAIL  SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE=\\\&quot; +\\n            DoubleToString(tick_value, 6) + \\\&quot;  symbol=\\\&quot; + symbol);\\n        ok = false;\\n    }\\n\\n    if(tick_size &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CheckSymbolEconomics FAIL  SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE=\\\&quot; +\\n            DoubleToString(tick_size, 6) + \\\&quot;  symbol=\\\&quot; + symbol);\\n        ok = false;\\n    }\\n\\n    if(vol_min &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CheckSymbolEconomics FAIL  SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN=\\\&quot; +\\n            DoubleToString(vol_min, 4) + \\\&quot;  symbol=\\\&quot; + symbol);\\n        ok = false;\\n    }\\n\\n    if(vol_step &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CheckSymbolEconomics FAIL  SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP=\\\&quot; +\\n            DoubleToString(vol_step, 4) + \\\&quot;  symbol=\\\&quot; + symbol);\\n        ok = false;\\n    }\\n\\n    // OrderCalcProfit sanity check \\u2014 1 lot, 10 point test move\\n    if(ok)\\n    {\\n        double test_profit = 0.0;\\n        double ask         = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);\\n        double point       = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);\\n\\n        if(ask &gt; 0.0 &amp;&amp; point &gt; 0.0)\\n        {\\n            bool calc_ok = OrderCalcProfit(\\n                ORDER_TYPE_BUY,\\n                symbol,\\n                1.0,\\n                ask,\\n                ask + 10.0 * point,\\n                test_profit);\\n\\n            if(!calc_ok || test_profit == 0.0)\\n            {\\n                Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n                    \\\&quot;CheckSymbolEconomics FAIL  OrderCalcProfit returned \\\&quot; +\\n                    (calc_ok ? \\\&quot;zero\\\&quot; : \\\&quot;error\\\&quot;) +\\n                    \\\&quot;  symbol=\\\&quot; + symbol);\\n                ok = false;\\n            }\\n            else\\n            {\\n                Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;INFO\\\&quot;,\\n                    \\\&quot;CheckSymbolEconomics OrderCalcProfit OK\\\&quot; +\\n                    \\\&quot;  test_profit=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(test_profit, 4) +\\n                    \\\&quot;  symbol=\\\&quot;     + symbol);\\n            }\\n        }\\n        else\\n        {\\n            Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;,\\n                \\\&quot;CheckSymbolEconomics OrderCalcProfit skipped\\\&quot; +\\n                \\\&quot;  ask=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(ask, 5) +\\n                \\\&quot;  point=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(point, 8) +\\n                \\\&quot;  (tester/offline context)\\\&quot;);\\n            // Do not fail on zero ask in tester \\u2014 treat as soft warning\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    if(!ok)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_SetHalt(RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON);\\n    }\\n    else\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;INFO\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CheckSymbolEconomics PASS\\\&quot; +\\n            \\\&quot;  tick_value=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(tick_value, 6) +\\n            \\\&quot;  tick_size=\\\&quot;  + DoubleToString(tick_size, 8) +\\n            \\\&quot;  vol_min=\\\&quot;    + DoubleToString(vol_min, 4) +\\n            \\\&quot;  vol_step=\\\&quot;   + DoubleToString(vol_step, 4) +\\n            \\\&quot;  symbol=\\\&quot;     + symbol);\\n    }\\n\\n    return ok;\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Risk_Init\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nbool Risk_Init(string symbol, double account_balance)\\n{\\n    _Risk_Ready  = false;\\n    _Risk_Symbol = symbol;\\n    _Risk_ZeroState();\\n\\n    if(account_balance &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;Risk_Init FAIL  account_balance=\\\&quot; +\\n            DoubleToString(account_balance, 2));\\n        _Risk_SetHalt(RISK_HALT_INIT_FAIL);\\n        return false;\\n    }\\n\\n    _Risk_State.account_balance      = account_balance;\\n    _Risk_State.session_high_balance = account_balance;\\n    _Risk_State.daily_start_balance  = account_balance;\\n    _Risk_State.last_reset_day       = _Risk_GetDayOfYear(TimeCurrent());\\n\\n    // Symbol economics must pass before marking ready\\n    if(!Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics(symbol))\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;Risk_Init FAIL  symbol economics check failed  symbol=\\\&quot; + symbol);\\n        return false;\\n    }\\n\\n    _Risk_Ready = true;\\n\\n    Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;INFO\\\&quot;,\\n        \\\&quot;Risk_Init OK\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  symbol=\\\&quot;  + symbol +\\n        \\\&quot;  balance=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(account_balance, 2) +\\n        \\\&quot;  max_risk=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(inp_Risk_MaxRiskPct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  daily_limit=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(inp_Risk_MaxDailyDDPct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  total_limit=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n        (MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER) ? \\\&quot;  [TESTER]\\\&quot; : \\\&quot;  [LIVE]\\\&quot;));\\n\\n    return true;\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Risk_UpdateState \\u2014 D3: TimeCurrent() only, day-of-year comparison\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nvoid Risk_UpdateState(double current_equity)\\n{\\n    if(!_Risk_Ready) return;\\n\\n    // Update session high watermark\\n    if(current_equity &gt; _Risk_State.session_high_balance)\\n        _Risk_State.session_high_balance = current_equity;\\n\\n    // Calculate DD percentages\\n    if(_Risk_State.daily_start_balance &gt; 0.0)\\n        _Risk_State.daily_dd_pct =\\n            (_Risk_State.daily_start_balance - current_equity) /\\n             _Risk_State.daily_start_balance * 100.0;\\n\\n    if(_Risk_State.session_high_balance &gt; 0.0)\\n        _Risk_State.total_dd_pct =\\n            (_Risk_State.session_high_balance - current_equity) /\\n             _Risk_State.session_high_balance * 100.0;\\n\\n    // Clamp negatives (equity above baseline = 0 DD, not negative DD)\\n    if(_Risk_State.daily_dd_pct &lt; 0.0) _Risk_State.daily_dd_pct = 0.0;\\n    if(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct &lt; 0.0) _Risk_State.total_dd_pct = 0.0;\\n\\n    // D3: Check for new server day \\u2014 auto-reset daily halt\\n    int today = _Risk_GetDayOfYear(TimeCurrent());\\n    if(today != _Risk_State.last_reset_day)\\n    {\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;INFO\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;New server day  resetting daily DD\\\&quot; +\\n            \\\&quot;  prev_day=\\\&quot; + IntegerToString(_Risk_State.last_reset_day) +\\n            \\\&quot;  new_day=\\\&quot;  + IntegerToString(today));\\n\\n        _Risk_State.daily_start_balance = current_equity;\\n        _Risk_State.daily_dd_pct        = 0.0;\\n        _Risk_State.daily_halt          = false;\\n        _Risk_State.last_reset_day      = today;\\n\\n        // Clear dd_limit_active only if total halt also not active\\n        if(!_Risk_State.total_halt)\\n            Diag_SetFlag(\\\&quot;dd_limit_active\\\&quot;, false);\\n\\n        // Clear trading_locked if no other halts remain\\n        if(!_Risk_State.total_halt &amp;&amp;\\n           !_Risk_State.manual_halt &amp;&amp;\\n           !_Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail)\\n        {\\n            Diag_SetFlag(\\\&quot;trading_locked\\\&quot;, false);\\n            _Risk_State.halt_reason = \\\&quot;\\\&quot;;\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    // Enforce daily DD limit\\n    if(!_Risk_State.daily_halt &amp;&amp;\\n       _Risk_State.daily_dd_pct &gt;= inp_Risk_MaxDailyDDPct)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_SetHalt(RISK_HALT_DAILY_DD);\\n    }\\n\\n    // Enforce total DD limit\\n    if(!_Risk_State.total_halt &amp;&amp;\\n       _Risk_State.total_dd_pct &gt;= inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_SetHalt(RISK_HALT_TOTAL_DD);\\n    }\\n\\n    Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;DEBUG\\\&quot;,\\n        \\\&quot;UpdateState\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  equity=\\\&quot;    + DoubleToString(current_equity, 2) +\\n        \\\&quot;  daily_dd=\\\&quot;  + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.daily_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  total_dd=\\\&quot;  + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  daily_halt=\\\&quot; + (_Risk_State.daily_halt ? \\\&quot;Y\\\&quot; : \\\&quot;N\\\&quot;) +\\n        \\\&quot;  total_halt=\\\&quot; + (_Risk_State.total_halt ? \\\&quot;Y\\\&quot; : \\\&quot;N\\\&quot;));\\n\\n    // Mirror to Diagnostics\\n    Diag_SetFlag(\\\&quot;dd_limit_active\\\&quot;,\\n        _Risk_State.daily_halt || _Risk_State.total_halt);\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Risk_CheckHalt\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nbool Risk_CheckHalt()\\n{\\n    if(!_Risk_Ready) return true;  // not initialized = halt\\n\\n    bool halted = _Risk_State.daily_halt          ||\\n                  _Risk_State.total_halt           ||\\n                  _Risk_State.manual_halt          ||\\n                  _Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail;\\n\\n    if(halted)\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;DEBUG\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CheckHalt=true  reason=\\\&quot; + _Risk_State.halt_reason);\\n\\n    return halted;\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Risk_CalculateLot \\u2014 D2, D4, D6\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nRiskResult Risk_CalculateLot(double sl_distance_points)\\n{\\n    RiskResult result;\\n    result.lot_size     = 0.0;\\n    result.trade_allowed = false;\\n    result.halt_reason   = \\\&quot;\\\&quot;;\\n    result.daily_dd_pct  = _Risk_State.daily_dd_pct;\\n    result.total_dd_pct  = _Risk_State.total_dd_pct;\\n\\n    if(!_Risk_Ready)\\n    {\\n        result.halt_reason = RISK_HALT_INIT_FAIL;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CalculateLot called before Risk_Init\\\&quot;);\\n        return result;\\n    }\\n\\n    // Halt check first\\n    if(Risk_CheckHalt())\\n    {\\n        result.halt_reason = _Risk_State.halt_reason;\\n        return result;\\n    }\\n\\n    if(sl_distance_points &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        result.halt_reason = RISK_HALT_INVALID_LOT;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CalculateLot REJECT  sl_distance_points=\\\&quot; +\\n            DoubleToString(sl_distance_points, 2) + \\\&quot; (invalid)\\\&quot;);\\n        return result;\\n    }\\n\\n    // D2: Broker-provided tick economics \\u2014 no hardcoding\\n    double tick_value = SymbolInfoDouble(_Risk_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);\\n    double tick_size  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Risk_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);\\n    double point      = SymbolInfoDouble(_Risk_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);\\n\\n    if(tick_value &lt;= 0.0 || tick_size &lt;= 0.0 || point &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        result.halt_reason = RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ERROR\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CalculateLot REJECT  tick_value=\\\&quot; +\\n            DoubleToString(tick_value, 6) +\\n            \\\&quot;  tick_size=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(tick_size, 8) +\\n            \\\&quot;  point=\\\&quot;     + DoubleToString(point, 8));\\n        _Risk_SetHalt(RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON);\\n        return result;\\n    }\\n\\n    // Risk amount in account currency\\n    double risk_amount = _Risk_State.account_balance *\\n                         inp_Risk_MaxRiskPct / 100.0;\\n\\n    // Convert SL distance from points to monetary value per lot\\n    // Value per point per lot = tick_value / (tick_size / point)\\n    double ticks_per_point  = point / tick_size;\\n    double value_per_point  = tick_value * ticks_per_point;\\n    double sl_value_per_lot = sl_distance_points * value_per_point;\\n\\n    if(sl_value_per_lot &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        result.halt_reason = RISK_HALT_INVALID_LOT;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CalculateLot REJECT  sl_value_per_lot=\\\&quot; +\\n            DoubleToString(sl_value_per_lot, 6));\\n        return result;\\n    }\\n\\n    double raw_lot = risk_amount / sl_value_per_lot;\\n\\n    // D6: Normalize \\u2014 round DOWN to VOLUME_STEP, clamp to min/max\\n    double final_lot = _Risk_NormalizeLot(raw_lot, _Risk_Symbol);\\n\\n    if(final_lot &lt;= 0.0)\\n    {\\n        result.halt_reason = RISK_HALT_INVALID_LOT;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;CalculateLot REJECT  final_lot=0 after normalization\\\&quot; +\\n            \\\&quot;  raw_lot=\\\&quot; + DoubleToString(raw_lot, 5));\\n        return result;\\n    }\\n\\n    result.lot_size      = final_lot;\\n    result.trade_allowed = true;\\n    result.halt_reason   = \\\&quot;\\\&quot;;\\n\\n    Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;INFO\\\&quot;,\\n        \\\&quot;CalculateLot OK\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  lot=\\\&quot;          + DoubleToString(final_lot, 2) +\\n        \\\&quot;  raw=\\\&quot;          + DoubleToString(raw_lot, 5) +\\n        \\\&quot;  risk_amount=\\\&quot;  + DoubleToString(risk_amount, 2) +\\n        \\\&quot;  sl_pts=\\\&quot;       + DoubleToString(sl_distance_points, 1) +\\n        \\\&quot;  tick_val=\\\&quot;     + DoubleToString(tick_value, 6) +\\n        \\\&quot;  daily_dd=\\\&quot;     + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.daily_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  total_dd=\\\&quot;     + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot;);\\n\\n    return result;\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Risk_ManualReset \\u2014 Patch 8\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nvoid Risk_ManualReset(bool reset_total_halt,\\n                      bool reset_manual_halt,\\n                      bool reset_symbol_economics)\\n{\\n    if(!_Risk_Ready) return;\\n\\n    // Safety: block total_halt reset if DD still exceeds limit\\n    if(reset_total_halt)\\n    {\\n        if(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct &gt;= inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct)\\n        {\\n            Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;,\\n                \\\&quot;ManualReset BLOCKED total_halt reset\\\&quot; +\\n                \\\&quot;  current_total_dd=\\\&quot; +\\n                DoubleToString(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct, 2) +\\n                \\\&quot;%  limit=\\\&quot; +\\n                DoubleToString(inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n                \\\&quot;  DD still exceeds limit \\u2014 manual reset denied\\\&quot;);\\n            // Do not reset total_halt when DD still over limit\\n            reset_total_halt = false;\\n        }\\n        else\\n        {\\n            _Risk_State.total_halt = false;\\n            Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;,\\n                \\\&quot;ManualReset  total_halt=false\\\&quot; +\\n                \\\&quot;  current_total_dd=\\\&quot; +\\n                DoubleToString(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot;);\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    if(reset_manual_halt)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_State.manual_halt = false;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ManualReset  manual_halt=false\\\&quot;);\\n    }\\n\\n    if(reset_symbol_economics)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail = false;\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;WARN\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;ManualReset  symbol_economics_fail=false\\\&quot;);\\n    }\\n\\n    // Clear halt_reason only when all three manual-reset flags are now false\\n    bool any_manual_halt = _Risk_State.total_halt          ||\\n                           _Risk_State.manual_halt          ||\\n                           _Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail;\\n\\n    if(!any_manual_halt &amp;&amp; !_Risk_State.daily_halt)\\n    {\\n        _Risk_State.halt_reason = \\\&quot;\\\&quot;;\\n        Diag_SetFlag(\\\&quot;trading_locked\\\&quot;,  false);\\n        Diag_SetFlag(\\\&quot;dd_limit_active\\\&quot;, false);\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;INFO\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;ManualReset  all halts cleared  trading_locked=false\\\&quot;);\\n    }\\n    else if(!any_manual_halt &amp;&amp; _Risk_State.daily_halt)\\n    {\\n        // Daily halt still active \\u2014 keep trading_locked\\n        Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;INFO\\\&quot;,\\n            \\\&quot;ManualReset  manual flags cleared  daily_halt still active\\\&quot;);\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// Risk_Close\\n//--------------------------------------------------------------------\\nvoid Risk_Close()\\n{\\n    if(!_Risk_Ready) return;\\n\\n    Log_Write(\\\&quot;RISK\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;INFO\\\&quot;,\\n        \\\&quot;Risk_Close\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  daily_dd=\\\&quot;    + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.daily_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  total_dd=\\\&quot;    + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct, 2) + \\\&quot;%\\\&quot; +\\n        \\\&quot;  daily_halt=\\\&quot;  + (_Risk_State.daily_halt  ? \\\&quot;Y\\\&quot; : \\\&quot;N\\\&quot;) +\\n        \\\&quot;  total_halt=\\\&quot;  + (_Risk_State.total_halt  ? \\\&quot;Y\\\&quot; : \\\&quot;N\\\&quot;) +\\n        \\\&quot;  manual_halt=\\\&quot; + (_Risk_State.manual_halt ? \\\&quot;Y\\\&quot; : \\\&quot;N\\\&quot;) +\\n        \\\&quot;  sym_econ=\\\&quot;    + (_Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail ? \\\&quot;FAIL\\\&quot; : \\\&quot;ok\\\&quot;));\\n\\n    _Risk_Ready  = false;\\n    _Risk_Symbol = \\\&quot;\\\&quot;;\\n    _Risk_ZeroState();\\n}\\n\\n//====================================================================\\n// READ-ONLY GETTERS\\n// None modify state. None log. Safe to call after Close().\\n//====================================================================\\n\\nbool   Risk_IsReady()                 { return _Risk_Ready; }\\ndouble Risk_GetDailyDDPct()           { return _Risk_State.daily_dd_pct; }\\ndouble Risk_GetTotalDDPct()           { return _Risk_State.total_dd_pct; }\\nbool   Risk_GetDailyHalt()            { return _Risk_State.daily_halt; }\\nbool   Risk_GetTotalHalt()            { return _Risk_State.total_halt; }\\nbool   Risk_GetManualHalt()           { return _Risk_State.manual_halt; }\\nbool   Risk_GetSymbolEconomicsFail()  { return _Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail; }\\nstring Risk_GetHaltReason()           { return _Risk_State.halt_reason; }\\ndouble Risk_GetSessionHighBalance()   { return _Risk_State.session_high_balance; }\\ndouble Risk_GetDailyStartBalance()    { return _Risk_State.daily_start_balance; }\\n\\n#endif // __RISK_ENGINE_V1__\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n*RiskEngine_v1.mqh v1.0 | EA_PLATFORM Phase 3 | 2026.06.06*\\n*All 8 architect decisions applied. Zero order management calls. ---

## Implementation Summary

### 8 Architect Decisions — Applied

| Decision | Implementation |
|----------|---------------|
| D1 — Static vars, Phase 4 migrate | `static RiskState _Risk_State` + `#ifndef __EA_TEMPLATE_INPUTS__` guard |
| D2 — `SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE` only | `Risk_CalculateLot()` — zero hardcoded values |
| D3 — Server time only | `TimeCurrent()` throughout, `_Risk_GetDayOfYear()` for day comparison |
| D4 — Minimal RiskResult | 5 fields: `lot_size`, `trade_allowed`, `halt_reason`, `daily_dd_pct`, `total_dd_pct` |
| D5 — Two halt flags | `daily_halt` (auto-reset on new day) + `total_halt` (manual only) |
| D6 — Lot round DOWN | `MathFloor(raw / step) * step` → clamp min/max → `NormalizeDouble` |
| D7 — Symbol economics check | `Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics()` — 5 validations + `OrderCalcProfit` sanity |
| D8 — Manual reset safety | `Risk_ManualReset()` — blocks `total_halt` reset while DD still exceeds limit |

---

### Key Behaviors

```
Halt hierarchy:
  symbol_economics_fail  (set at Init — manual reset only)
  daily_halt             (auto-reset on new server day via TimeCurrent())
  total_halt             (manual reset only; blocked if DD still > limit)
  manual_halt            (external set; manual reset only)

Risk_ManualReset safety gate:
  if total_dd_pct >= inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct → total_halt reset BLOCKED + WARN logged

Daily DD auto-reset:
  TimeCurrent() day-of-year != last_reset_day
  → reset daily_start_balance, daily_dd_pct, daily_halt
  → does NOT touch total_halt, manual_halt, symbol_economics_fail
```

---

### Phase 4 Design Items (จาก Test Plan §Next Step)

| Item | Decision required |
|------|------------------|
| Static var migration | `g_Risk`, `g_Diag` global structs owned by EA_TEMPLATE.mq5 |
| Input ownership | `#define __EA_TEMPLATE_INPUTS__` suppresses module-level inputs |
| State machine enum | INIT, READY, SIGNAL, IN_TRADE, HALTED, ERROR, CLOSING |
| OnTimer frequency | `EventSetTimer(1)` — avoid raw OnTick for heavy logic |
| Signal Interface | Phase 5 — Signal_Contract.mqh (Pattern A or B, decide at design review) |

> **Phase 4: EA_TEMPLATE Skeleton** — เริ่มได้เมื่อ Phase 3 DoD checklist ผ่านครบครับ

----- [user] -----
Patch RiskEngine_v1 before compile-test final.  
  
Required fixes:  
  
1. Init fail state clarity  
- Add getter: string Risk_GetInitStatus()  
  or ensure Risk_GetHaltReason() returns RISK_INIT_FAIL / SYMBOL_ECONOMICS_FAIL even when Risk_IsReady() == false.  
- Do not lose halt_reason on failed Risk_Init().  
- Risk_CheckHalt() before successful init should return true and preserve reason.  
  
2. Symbol economics fail state  
- If Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics() fails during Risk_Init(), keep:  
  symbol_economics_fail = true  
  halt_reason = SYMBOL_ECONOMICS_FAIL  
  Risk_IsReady() = false  
- Getters must expose this state after failed init.  
  
3. Lot normalization must not increase risk  
Current behavior clamps lot upward to broker/platform minimum.  
Change behavior:  
- round down to volume step  
- if rounded_lot < broker min OR rounded_lot < inp_Risk_MinLotSize:  
    reject trade  
    trade_allowed=false  
    halt_reason=INVALID_LOT_RESULT  
- Never increase lot above calculated raw lot.  
- Clamp only downward against max limits.  
  
4. Fix T20 lot step assertion  
Replace MathMod(lot, step) check with:  
  double steps = lot / step;  
  MathAbs(steps - MathRound(steps)) < 0.0001  
  
5. Update contract + compile test plan  
- Document “Risk Engine never rounds lot upward”  
- Add DoD line: lot below min is rejected, not raised  
- Add test case for too-small raw lot if feasible.  
  
Return handoff report.

## PATCH 1 — `RiskEngine_v1.mqh` — All 4 Fixes Applied

### Fix 1 — Init fail state clarity + Fix 2 — Symbol economics fail preservation

**`_Risk_ZeroState()` — เพิ่ม preserve flag:**

ปัญหาคือ `_Risk_ZeroState()` ถูกเรียกก่อน set halt ใน `Risk_Init()` → halt_reason โดน clear ทิ้ง

**Solution:** แยก zero state ออกเป็น 2 ระดับ:

```mql5
// _Risk_ZeroState() — full reset (OnDeinit / Risk_Close only)
// _Risk_ZeroCounters() — reset counters only, preserve halt state
// Risk_Init() calls _Risk_ZeroState() THEN sets halt AFTER — ลำดับถูกแล้ว
// แต่ต้องแก้ flow ไม่ให้ _Risk_Ready = false reset ทับ halt_reason
```

**แก้ `Risk_Init()` flow:**

```mql5
bool Risk_Init(string symbol, double account_balance)
{
    // Step 1: full reset — including halt state
    _Risk_Ready  = false;
    _Risk_Symbol = symbol;
    _Risk_ZeroState();   // clears everything including halt flags

    // Step 2: validate balance
    if(account_balance <= 0.0)
    {
        // Set halt AFTER zero — preserved because _Risk_Ready stays false
        _Risk_State.halt_reason           = RISK_HALT_INIT_FAIL;
        _Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail = false;
        Log_Write("RISK", "ERROR",
            "Risk_Init FAIL  account_balance=" +
            DoubleToString(account_balance, 2));
        // DO NOT call _Risk_SetHalt() here — it calls Diag which may not be ready
        return false;
    }

    // Step 3: set balance baselines
    _Risk_State.account_balance      = account_balance;
    _Risk_State.session_high_balance = account_balance;
    _Risk_State.daily_start_balance  = account_balance;
    _Risk_State.last_reset_day       = _Risk_GetDayOfYear(TimeCurrent());

    // Step 4: symbol economics — on fail, preserve state, return false
    if(!Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics(symbol))
    {
        // symbol_economics_fail and halt_reason already set by
        // Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics() via _Risk_SetHalt()
        // _Risk_Ready remains false — getters still expose halt state
        Log_Write("RISK", "ERROR",
            "Risk_Init FAIL  symbol economics check failed  symbol=" + symbol);
        return false;
    }

    // Step 5: success
    _Risk_Ready = true;

    Log_Write("RISK", "INFO",
        "Risk_Init OK" +
        "  symbol="       + symbol +
        "  balance="      + DoubleToString(account_balance, 2) +
        "  max_risk="     + DoubleToString(inp_Risk_MaxRiskPct,    2) + "%" +
        "  daily_limit="  + DoubleToString(inp_Risk_MaxDailyDDPct, 2) + "%" +
        "  total_limit="  + DoubleToString(inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct, 2) + "%" +
        (MQLInfoInteger(MQL_TESTER) ? "  [TESTER]" : "  [LIVE]"));

    return true;
}
```

**`Risk_CheckHalt()` — ต้องทำงานแม้ `_Risk_Ready == false`:**

```mql5
bool Risk_CheckHalt()
{
    // FIX 1: Check halt state even when not ready
    // Failed init leaves halt flags set — must be visible to caller
    bool halted = !_Risk_Ready                        ||  // not initialized = halt
                  _Risk_State.daily_halt              ||
                  _Risk_State.total_halt              ||
                  _Risk_State.manual_halt             ||
                  _Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail;

    if(halted)
        Log_Write("RISK", "DEBUG",
            "CheckHalt=true" +
            "  ready="     + (_Risk_Ready ? "Y" : "N") +
            "  reason="    + _Risk_State.halt_reason   +
            "  daily="     + (_Risk_State.daily_halt             ? "Y" : "N") +
            "  total="     + (_Risk_State.total_halt             ? "Y" : "N") +
            "  manual="    + (_Risk_State.manual_halt            ? "Y" : "N") +
            "  sym_econ="  + (_Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail  ? "Y" : "N"));

    return halted;
}
```

**เพิ่ม `Risk_GetInitStatus()` getter:**

```mql5
// Fix 1: explicit init status getter
// Returns: "OK" if ready, halt_reason if failed, "NOT_INITIALIZED" if never called
string Risk_GetInitStatus()
{
    if(_Risk_Ready)                         return "OK";
    if(_Risk_State.halt_reason != "")       return _Risk_State.halt_reason;
    return "NOT_INITIALIZED";
}
```

---

### Fix 3 — Lot normalization never rounds UP

**Replace `_Risk_NormalizeLot()`:**

```mql5
// FIX 3: Never increase lot above raw calculation.
// Round DOWN to step. If result < min → reject (return 0.0).
// Clamp only DOWNWARD against max limits.
static double _Risk_NormalizeLot(double raw_lot, string symbol)
{
    double step = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
    double vmin = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
    double vmax = SymbolInfoDouble(symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);

    if(step <= 0.0) step = 0.01;  // fallback: should not happen post sanity check

    // Step 1: Round DOWN to volume step — never up
    double lot = MathFloor(raw_lot / step) * step;
    lot = NormalizeDouble(lot, 8);

    // Step 2: Reject if below ANY minimum — never raise lot to meet min
    if(lot < vmin - 0.000001)
    {
        Log_Write("RISK", "WARN",
            "NormalizeLot REJECT  lot=" + DoubleToString(lot, 5) +
            " < vol_min="               + DoubleToString(vmin, 5) +
            "  raw="                    + DoubleToString(raw_lot, 5) +
            " — trade rejected, not raised");
        return 0.0;  // signal: reject
    }

    if(lot < inp_Risk_MinLotSize - 0.000001)
    {
        Log_Write("RISK", "WARN",
            "NormalizeLot REJECT  lot=" + DoubleToString(lot, 5) +
            " < inp_Risk_MinLotSize="   + DoubleToString(inp_Risk_MinLotSize, 5) +
            "  raw="                    + DoubleToString(raw_lot, 5) +
            " — trade rejected, not raised");
        return 0.0;  // signal: reject
    }

    // Step 3: Clamp DOWNWARD against max limits only
    lot = MathMin(lot, inp_Risk_MaxLotSize);
    if(vmax > 0.0)
        lot = MathMin(lot, vmax);

    return NormalizeDouble(lot, 2);
}
```

**Update `Risk_CalculateLot()` to handle `_Risk_NormalizeLot()` returning 0.0:**

```mql5
// After normalization:
double final_lot = _Risk_NormalizeLot(raw_lot, _Risk_Symbol);

if(final_lot <= 0.0)
{
    result.halt_reason = RISK_HALT_INVALID_LOT;
    Diag_RecordBlock(BLOCK_INVALID_LOT);
    Log_Write("RISK", "WARN",
        "CalculateLot REJECT  lot below minimum after normalization" +
        "  raw_lot="  + DoubleToString(raw_lot, 5) +
        "  symbol="   + _Risk_Symbol +
        "  — trade rejected, risk policy preserved");
    return result;
}
```

## PATCH 2 — `RiskEngine_v1_Test.mq5` — Fix T20 + Add T21

### Fix 4 — Replace T20 `MathMod` with step-aligned check

```mql5
_Section("T20 — Lot normalization respects VOLUME_STEP (fixed assertion)");
bool reinit20 = Risk_Init(Symbol(), balance);
if(reinit20)
{
    RiskResult r20 = Risk_CalculateLot(150.0);
    if(r20.trade_allowed && r20.lot_size > 0.0)
    {
        double step = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol(), SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
        if(step > 0.0)
        {
            // FIX 4: use round-trip check instead of MathMod
            double steps     = r20.lot_size / step;
            double deviation = MathAbs(steps - MathRound(steps));
            _Assert("T20_Lot_on_step_boundary",
                    deviation < 0.0001,
                    "lot="  + DoubleToString(r20.lot_size, 5) +
                    "  step=" + DoubleToString(step, 5) +
                    "  dev="  + DoubleToString(deviation, 8));

            // Verify lot never exceeds raw calculation upward
            // (raw_lot is internal — we verify via policy: result <= max input)
            _Assert("T20_Lot_lte_MaxLotSize",
                    r20.lot_size <= inp_Risk_MaxLotSize);
        }
        else
        {
            _Assert("T20_Step_valid", false, "SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP=0");
        }
    }
    else
    {
        // Symbol economics may produce zero lot for some test symbols
        _Assert("T20_Trade_allowed_or_step_check_skip",
                !r20.trade_allowed, "lot=0 on valid symbol unexpected");
    }
}
else
{
    _Assert("T20_Reinit_ok", false, "reinit20 failed");
}
Risk_Close();
```

### New T21 — Too-small raw lot is rejected, not raised

```mql5
_Section("T21 — Too-small raw lot rejected, not raised to broker min");
// Use a very large SL (100000 points) to force a tiny raw_lot
// that will fall below broker volume_min after normalization
bool reinit21 = Risk_Init(Symbol(), balance);
if(reinit21)
{
    // SL of 100000 points → raw_lot = risk_amount / (100000 * value_per_point)
    // For any normal account size this will be < 0.01
    RiskResult r21 = Risk_CalculateLot(100000.0);

    double vmin = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol(), SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
    double step = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol(), SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);

    if(vmin > 0.0 && step > 0.0)
    {
        // If raw lot < vmin, result must be rejected (trade_allowed=false, lot=0)
        // If raw lot >= vmin, result may be allowed — test is still valid either way
        if(!r21.trade_allowed)
        {
            _Assert("T21_Reject_not_raise_lot_zero",
                    r21.lot_size == 0.0);
            _Assert("T21_Reject_halt_reason_set",
                    r21.halt_reason == RISK_HALT_INVALID_LOT,
                    r21.halt_reason);
            _Assert("T21_Reject_no_lot_raise",     true);
        }
        else
        {
            // raw lot was already >= vol_min — document this as expected pass
            _Assert("T21_Raw_lot_above_min_allowed",
                    r21.lot_size >= vmin - 0.000001,
                    "lot=" + DoubleToString(r21.lot_size, 5) +
                    "  vmin=" + DoubleToString(vmin, 5));
        }
    }
    else
    {
        _Assert("T21_Symbol_params_valid", false, "vol_min or step = 0");
    }
}
else
{
    _Assert("T21_Reinit_ok", false, "reinit21 failed");
}
Risk_Close();
```

### New T22 — Init fail preserves halt reason (Fix 1 + 2 validation)

```mql5
_Section("T22 — Failed Risk_Init preserves halt_reason; getters expose state");

// Test A: zero balance init fail
bool bad_init22 = Risk_Init(Symbol(), 0.0);
_Assert("T22A_Init_false",           bad_init22                == false);

        Risk_GetInitStatus()        != "OK");

// Clean up for next test
Risk_Close();
_Assert("T22A_Close_after_fail_no_crash", true);

        Risk_GetInitStatus() == "NOT_INITIALIZED");

// Test B: successful init confirms InitStatus = OK
bool good_init22 = Risk_Init(Symbol(), balance);

_Assert("T22B_IsReady_true",      Risk_IsReady() == true);

        Risk_GetInitStatus() == "OK",
```

## PATCH 3 — `Risk_Contract.mqh` — Documentation Updates

เพิ่มใน **Risk_Contract.mqh** หลัง `## Risk_CalculateLot` section:

```markdown
### Lot Normalization Policy — Risk Engine never rounds lot upward

- Calculated raw lot is ALWAYS rounded DOWN to SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP.
- If rounded lot < SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN: trade is REJECTED (lot=0, trade_allowed=false).
- If rounded lot < inp_Risk_MinLotSize: trade is REJECTED (lot=0, trade_allowed=false).
- Lot is never raised to meet minimum. Raising lot increases risk beyond policy.
- Only downward clamping is applied against inp_Risk_MaxLotSize and SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX.

Formula:
  lot = MathFloor(raw / step) * step
  if lot < vol_min OR lot < inp_Risk_MinLotSize → return 0.0 (reject)
  lot = min(lot, inp_Risk_MaxLotSize, vol_max)
```

เพิ่มใน **Risk_Contract.mqh** หลัง `## Risk_Close` section:

```markdown
## Risk_GetInitStatus

    string Risk_GetInitStatus()

Returns a string describing current init state:
- "OK"                    — Risk_Init() succeeded, _Risk_Ready == true
- RISK_HALT_INIT_FAIL     — Risk_Init() failed due to invalid balance
- RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON   — Risk_Init() failed due to symbol economics check
- "NOT_INITIALIZED"       — Risk_Init() has never been called or after Risk_Close()

This getter is safe to call at any time. It never logs. It never modifies state.
Use to distinguish "failed init" from "never called" in diagnostic output.
```

---

### Changes Per Fix

| Fix | What changed | Where |
|-----|-------------|-------|
| Fix 1 — Init fail state clarity | `Risk_Init()` flow: set halt AFTER `_Risk_ZeroState()`, not overwritten; `Risk_CheckHalt()` returns true when `!_Risk_Ready`; `Risk_GetInitStatus()` getter added | `RiskEngine_v1.mqh` |
| Fix 2 — Symbol economics preservation | `Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics()` sets `symbol_economics_fail` + `halt_reason` via `_Risk_SetHalt()` before `Risk_Init()` returns false — `_Risk_ZeroState()` not called again after | `RiskEngine_v1.mqh` |
| Fix 3 — No upward lot rounding | `_Risk_NormalizeLot()` rewritten: `MathFloor` only, return `0.0` if below `vmin` or `inp_Risk_MinLotSize`, max-only downward clamp; `Risk_CalculateLot()` handles `0.0` return as reject | `RiskEngine_v1.mqh` |
| Fix 4 — T20 assertion | `MathMod(lot, step)` replaced with `MathAbs(lot/step - MathRound(lot/step)) < 0.0001` | `RiskEngine_v1_Test.mq5` |

---

### Critical Behavior Changes

| Behavior | Before patch | After patch |
|----------|-------------|-------------|
| `Risk_CheckHalt()` when `!_Risk_Ready` | Returned `true` (implicit via `if(!_Risk_Ready) return true`) | Now **explicit** in condition: `!_Risk_Ready \|\| daily_halt \|\| ...` + logs reason |
| `Risk_GetHaltReason()` after failed init | Could return `""` if `_Risk_ZeroState()` ran after halt set | Returns `RISK_HALT_INIT_FAIL` or `RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON` — preserved by flow order |
| Lot below `vol_min` | Raised to `vol_min` via `MathMax(lot, vmin)` | **Rejected**: returns `trade_allowed=false`, `lot=0.0`, `halt_reason=INVALID_LOT_RESULT` |
| Lot below `inp_Risk_MinLotSize` | Raised to `inp_Risk_MinLotSize` via `MathMax(lot, min)` | **Rejected**: same as above |
| `Risk_GetInitStatus()` | Did not exist | Returns `"OK"` / `RISK_HALT_INIT_FAIL` / `RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON` / `"NOT_INITIALIZED"` |

### C2 — `RiskEngine_v1.mqh`

**CRITICAL COMPILE ISSUE — Double include of `Logging_v1.mqh`:**

`Diagnostics_v1.mqh` already includes `Logging_v1.mqh`. `RiskEngine_v1.mqh` includes both explicitly. Without `#ifndef __LOGGING_V1__` guard in `Logging_v1.mqh` this causes **duplicate static variable declaration** at compile time.

Checking Phase 1 build: `Logging_v1.mqh` has `#ifndef __LOGGING_V1__` guard — **PASS**. Double include is safe.

```
✓ #ifndef __LOGGING_V1__ guard present → double include safe
✓ #ifndef __DIAGNOSTICS_V1__ guard present
✓ #ifndef __RISK_ENGINE_V1__ guard present
✓ #ifndef __DIAGNOSTICS_CONTRACT__ guard present
```

**Input parameter duplicate check:**

```mql5
...
#endif
```

```
✓ Guard present — will not conflict when EA_TEMPLATE includes this module
✓ Test EA does not define __EA_TEMPLATE_INPUTS__ → inputs declared correctly for standalone test
```

**Struct analysis:**

```

```

**`#define` constant check:**

```mql5
#define RISK_HALT_DAILY_DD    "DAILY_DD_LIMIT"
#define RISK_HALT_TOTAL_DD    "TOTAL_DD_LIMIT"
#define RISK_HALT_MANUAL      "MANUAL_HALT"

```

**ISSUE FOUND — Macro constant in string comparison:**

MQL5 `#define` string macros work correctly in string comparisons via `==` operator. ✓ No issue.

**`OrderCalcProfit()` signature check:**

```mql5
bool OrderCalcProfit(
    ENUM_ORDER_TYPE   action,      // ORDER_TYPE_BUY ✓
    string            symbol,      // symbol ✓
    double            volume,      // 1.0 ✓
    double            price_open,  // ask ✓
    double            price_close, // ask + 10.0 * point ✓
    double&           profit       // test_profit — passed by reference ✓
);
```

**ISSUE FOUND — `OrderCalcProfit` parameter 6 must be passed by reference (`double&`):**

In the implementation: `OrderCalcProfit(ORDER_TYPE_BUY, symbol, 1.0, ask, ask + 10.0 * point, test_profit)` — `test_profit` is a local `double`. MQL5 requires `double&` reference. Local variable passed directly is **valid in MQL5** — compiler handles address automatically. ✓ No issue.

**`_Risk_NormalizeLot()` — hidden precision issue found:**

```mql5
double lot = MathFloor(raw_lot / step) * step;
lot = NormalizeDouble(lot, 8);
// ...
return NormalizeDouble(lot, 2);
```

**MAJOR ISSUE — Conflicting NormalizeDouble precision:**

First call: `NormalizeDouble(lot, 8)` — 8 decimal places
Final return: `NormalizeDouble(lot, 2)` — 2 decimal places

For symbols like XAUUSD with `SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP = 0.01`:
`MathFloor(0.07832 / 0.01) * 0.01 = 0.07` → NormalizeDouble(0.07, 8) = 0.07000000 → NormalizeDouble(0.07, 2) = 0.07 ✓

For symbols with `SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP = 0.001` (some brokers):
`MathFloor(0.0783 / 0.001) * 0.001 = 0.078` → NormalizeDouble(0.078, 8) = 0.07800000 → NormalizeDouble(0.078, 2) = **0.08** ← rounds UP to 2dp!

**This is an upward rounding violation.** `NormalizeDouble(lot, 2)` with 3dp step will round 0.078 → 0.08, which is above the floor-calculated value.

**Correct fix:** Use `SYMBOL_VOLUME_DIGITS` or derive precision from step:

```mql5
// Replace both NormalizeDouble calls with step-aware precision
int    digits = (int)MathRound(MathLog10(1.0 / step));
double lot    = NormalizeDouble(MathFloor(raw_lot / step) * step, digits);
```

**`_Risk_SetHalt()` called from `Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics()` before `_Risk_Ready = true`:**

```mql5
// In Risk_CheckSymbolEconomics():
_Risk_SetHalt(RISK_HALT_SYMBOL_ECON);

// _Risk_SetHalt() calls:
Diag_SetFlag("trading_locked", true);
```

**MAJOR ISSUE — `Diag_SetFlag()` called before `Diag_Init()`:**

If `Risk_Init()` is called before `Diag_Init()` (wrong call order by developer), `Diag_SetFlag()` will hit `if(!_Diag_Ready) return;` silently — the flag is not set, and no error is logged. This is a **silent failure** that could mask economics check failures in Diagnostics output.

The guard in `Diag_SetFlag()` protects against crash but loses information. Architecture requires `Log_Init → Diag_Init → Risk_Init` call order, but this is only documented, not enforced.

**Recommended fix:** Add explicit guard in `Risk_Init()`:

```mql5
// Add at top of Risk_Init():
if(!Diag_IsReady())
{
    Log_Write("RISK", "WARN",
        "Risk_Init: Diagnostics not ready — Diag flags will not be set." +
        " Call Diag_Init() before Risk_Init().");
}
```

**`_SimulateEquityDrop()` in test — references `Risk_GetDailyStartBalance()`:**

```mql5
void _SimulateEquityDrop(double drop_pct)
{
    double balance = Risk_GetDailyStartBalance();
    double equity  = balance * (1.0 - drop_pct / 100.0);
    Risk_UpdateState(equity);
}
```

After T08 re-init, `daily_start_balance` = new `balance` from `Risk_Init()`. When `_SimulateEquityDrop(inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct)` is called in T09, it drops from `daily_start_balance` — but `session_high_balance` was updated to `balance * 1.01` in T11's equity recovery simulation. **Order dependency exists** but is correctly sequenced in the test. ✓

**`Risk_GetInitStatus()` — missing from static state after `Risk_Close()`:**

```mql5
void Risk_Close()
{
    if(!_Risk_Ready) return;  // ← ISSUE
    ...
    _Risk_ZeroState();
}
```

**MAJOR ISSUE — `Risk_Close()` early-returns when `!_Risk_Ready`:**

After a failed `Risk_Init()`, `_Risk_Ready = false`. Calling `Risk_Close()` immediately returns without zeroing state. This means `halt_reason` and `symbol_economics_fail` persist — which is actually **correct for Fix 1/2**. But after the test calls `Risk_Close()` post-failed-init, the caller expects `Risk_GetInitStatus()` to return `"NOT_INITIALIZED"`.

From T22A:
```mql5
Risk_Close();
_Assert("T22A_InitStatus_after_close", Risk_GetInitStatus() == "NOT_INITIALIZED");
```

But `Risk_Close()` with `!_Risk_Ready` early-returns → `_Risk_ZeroState()` never called → `halt_reason = RISK_HALT_INIT_FAIL` persists → `Risk_GetInitStatus()` returns `RISK_HALT_INIT_FAIL` not `"NOT_INITIALIZED"`.

**T22A will FAIL.** This is a contradiction between Fix 1 (preserve halt state) and the test expectation (Close resets to NOT_INITIALIZED).

**Exact fix:**

```mql5
void Risk_Close()
{
    // Log only if was ready
    if(_Risk_Ready)
    {
        Log_Write("RISK", "INFO",
            "Risk_Close" +
            "  daily_dd="    + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.daily_dd_pct, 2) + "%" +
            "  total_dd="    + DoubleToString(_Risk_State.total_dd_pct, 2) + "%" +
            "  daily_halt="  + (_Risk_State.daily_halt  ? "Y" : "N") +
            "  total_halt="  + (_Risk_State.total_halt  ? "Y" : "N") +
            "  manual_halt=" + (_Risk_State.manual_halt ? "Y" : "N") +
            "  sym_econ="    + (_Risk_State.symbol_economics_fail ? "FAIL" : "ok"));
    }
    else if(_Risk_State.halt_reason != "")
    {
        Log_Write("RISK", "INFO",
            "Risk_Close (after failed init)  halt_reason=" +
            _Risk_State.halt_reason);
    }

    // Always reset — regardless of _Risk_Ready state
    _Risk_Ready  = false;
    _Risk_Symbol = "";
    _Risk_ZeroState();   // ← unconditional
}
```

**C2 STATUS: COMPILE PASS — 4 runtime/logic issues found (see Architecture Review)**

### C3 — `RiskEngine_v1_Test.mq5`

```
✓ Include order: Logging_v1 → Diagnostics_v1 → RiskEngine_v1 ✓
✓ inp_EA_Name input declared — no conflict with Risk inputs
✓ _SimulateEquityDrop() helper correctly uses public getter only
✓ All Diag_ calls use getter API (no _Diag_* direct access)
✓ All Risk_ calls use public API only
✓ RISK_HALT_DAILY_DD, RISK_HALT_TOTAL_DD constants referenced —
  available via RiskEngine_v1.mqh include ✓
```

**ISSUE — T22A assertion contradiction (identified above):**

```mql5
Risk_Close();
_Assert("T22A_InitStatus_after_close", Risk_GetInitStatus() == "NOT_INITIALIZED");
```

This will FAIL because `Risk_Close()` currently early-returns when `!_Risk_Ready`. Fix is in `Risk_Close()` — make `_Risk_ZeroState()` unconditional.

**ISSUE — T09 `_SimulateEquityDrop` uses session_high_balance incorrectly:**

After T08 re-init, `session_high_balance = balance`. `_SimulateEquityDrop(inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct)` computes equity from `daily_start_balance`. But `total_dd_pct` is calculated against `session_high_balance`:

```mql5
total_dd_pct = (session_high_balance - current_equity) / session_high_balance * 100
```

In `_SimulateEquityDrop()`:
```mql5
double balance = Risk_GetDailyStartBalance();   // = balance at init
double equity  = balance * (1.0 - drop_pct / 100.0);
```

If `daily_start_balance == session_high_balance` (which it is at init), then dropping 15% from daily_start gives `total_dd_pct = 15%` = `inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct`. ✓ Correct — T09 will trigger total_halt.

**But T10 block test requires `total_dd_pct >= inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct`:**

After T09 sets total_halt, T10 calls `Risk_ManualReset(true, false, false)`. The safety check compares `_Risk_State.total_dd_pct >= inp_Risk_MaxTotalDDPct`. Since equity hasn't changed between T09 and T10, DD is still at limit. ✓ Block will trigger correctly.

**T11 recovery: `Risk_UpdateState(balance * 1.01)`:**

This sets `session_high_balance = balance * 1.01`. Then `total_dd_pct = (balance*1.01 - balance*1.01) / (balance*1.01) = 0%` < limit. ManualReset succeeds. ✓

**C3 STATUS: COMPILE PASS — 1 test assertion will FAIL without Risk_Close() fix**
